## 25. PROPOSED (owner direction 2026-11-05) — per-role hand-edit controls ("sheet latitude"), Honour mode flipped, inventory moves — not a go

(Design pass by a Plan agent, 2026-11-05, pasted by the lead; ★ for the owner below.)

**Today (checked):** `changedByRefusal` (`sockets/state.ts` ~317–336) sets writer = `gm` when the hand is the session owner, else `holder` — never checking the target is that person's; `changedByAllows` (`rulesets/scopes.ts` ~102–112): missing `changedBy` or `everyone` lets anyone; a slot no ruleset attribute files under (e.g. `inventory`, host-state slots) is open. `ownerEditable` (`state.ts` ~556–563): world, NPC cast and places are "play" (editable by guests); seats by the owner, or their player while `playerEditsOwnSheet` (`write.ts` ~1351: Honour mode `!== false`). A carded seat is "own" by the seat's user. Slot locks `none·people·all` (`write.ts` ~1366–1389) can be set by anyone passing `assertSessionOwner` (guests included). Watchers (`session_guests.is_player=false`) pass every "play" gate. Honour mode: only the Crawler declares it (`HONOR_MODE_FIELD`, default false; ON = players edit sheets + host deletes the latest turn).

### A. SDK vocabulary — 🚧 **sheet latitude**
`SHEET_LATITUDES = ['as-ruled','no-edits','all-own','all-any']` — one level per role: _As the rules say_ (owner: anyone's; player: own; `changedBy` with writer gm/holder) · _No editing by hand_ · _Everything on their own characters_ (all attributes, overrides `changedBy`) · _Everything on anyone's_ (plus world and places; overrides `changedBy`). "Own": for a player, cards with `characters.userId` = them plus their card-less seat; for the owner, their own cards plus the world and places. Override never passes a slot lock `all` or earshot. Not `changedBy`, not a slot lock, not `reach`. Declared as two shared fields core reads (the AUTO_ADVANCE precedent): `OWNER_SHEET_LATITUDE_FIELD`, `PLAYER_SHEET_LATITUDE_FIELD` (`enum`, group _Players_); a genre exposes one by placing it in `shape.fields` with its default, or pins it silently; the R32 cascade applies; one pure SDK judge `sheetLatitudeAllows(...)` for sheet and server. Watchers always `no-edits` (no setting). Examples: Story/Lair — owner `all-any` pinned, players `as-ruled` exposed; Dungeon Crawler — owner `as-ruled` pinned, players `no-edits` exposed, Honour mode declared default ON; Chat/Guide — core default; Mundi — owner `as-ruled` pinned.

### B. Server gate — one decider (P2)
New `state/handEdits.ts` `handEditRefusal(db, { sessionId, userId, owner, slotId, act: 'set'|'configure'|'take-from'|'put-on' })`: role (owner/player/watcher), the role's latitude, whose the target is, `changedByAllows` with the writer resolved properly, `lockAllows`, `valueHeard`. Runs never reach it. `configure`: only the session owner sets/clears a lock. Callers: `state:set` (replaces `changedByRefusal`'s hand role), `state:configure`, `givePiece` (each side) and offer accept, `describeState` (the sheet). Removes the hand blocks in `write.ts` (~1517–1529, ~1555–1568), `playerEditsOwnSheet`, `PLAYER_SHEET_REFUSAL`. Sheet facts: per-slot `handLocked?: Record<slotId, sentence>` (the lock shown with its cause, P1). Refusals kept + new: _Players don't edit sheets by hand in this session. The session owner can change that in the session's settings, under Players._ (pinned: _…in this genre._) · _You're watching this session; only its players and owner change sheets._ · _Only the session owner locks or unlocks a stat._

### C. Stored data / 0.6
A **data upgrade** on a new generated custom migration (runs once, ledgered — a plain boot pass would flip the value back on the second boot). `sessions.genre_fields.honorMode`: `true` → `honorMode:false` + `playerSheetLatitude:'as-ruled'`; `false` → `honorMode:true` + `'no-edits'`; absent → nothing (genre defaults). `session_presets.settings`: same, keeping the `{value, lock, askAtStart}` wrapper. Tests incl. second boot. `HONOR_MODE_FIELD` default **true**, description _Every turn is kept: nobody deletes the latest turn — retake it instead. Off: the host may delete the latest turn where deleting is on._; `turnDeleteVerdict` polarity flips; refusal _Honour mode is on: every turn is kept — retake from the latest instead. The session owner can turn it off in the session's settings._ Existing Crawler sessions: identical behaviour (except a guest seated on the owner's card). Changes for everyone, each shown by a refusal and a sheet lock: guests lose edits on NPCs/world/places; `holder` really means the holder; watchers lose everything; guests can't set/clear locks. Risk: export files and plugin preset settings written before the flip — the importer maps older files.

### D. Inventory moves
Two stores: the `inventory` list (`core:slot/inventory@1`, words or `{entryId,count}`, hand + **Give to…** `state/give.ts`) and zones (`rulesets/zones.ts`, rules only). Cases: **Give** seat→seat — keep, judged as a pair of edits (`take-from` giver, `put-on` receiver), offer when needed · **Drop** — giver's side only · **Pick up** — receiver's side only · **Loot** NPC→seat — rules or the owner's latitude · **Shop/Buy** — invoking is play, not a hand edit · **Trade/containers** — not built; two offers make a trade · **keeper deltas** — runs, unchanged. Missing (named, not fixed): `state:givePiece` skips `changedBy`/earshot checks; pick-up/drop don't check presence at the place; at `no-edits` a pick-up becomes an offer the player can't accept; a keeper give is two unlinked deltas (half-apply under Review); two stores of carried things; `changedBy` can't mark `inventory` (not a ruleset attribute).

### E. Lanes
1 **SDK** (sheetLatitude, `handLocked`, the two fields, `HONOR_MODE_FIELD` flipped, Story/Lair/DC declarations; `sdk:build`) · 2 **Marks** (VI all but Mood; Wherewithal three pools) beside 1 · 3 **ed** gate + data upgrade (after 1 and m3b-V2) · 4 **client and words** · 5 **port** (Mood/Age free, rest `['rules']`; owner `as-ruled` pinned).

### ★ for the owner
1 Shape and names (two shared fields + `handLocked`, vs a typed `GenreDecl.sheetLatitude`). 2 Defaults when a genre says nothing (owner and players `as-ruled`; Story/Lair pin owner `all-any`). 3 What "own"/"anyone" cover (world and places = the owner's; watchers `no-edits`). 4 How far override goes (passes `changedBy` incl. `rules`; never a lock `all` or earshot; only the owner locks). 5 How rulesets mark a locked attribute (`['rules']`, the GM's part left to latitude). 6 Boot pass as a data upgrade + import mapping. 7 Moves = a pair of edits through the decider; drop/pick-up one side; nothing new for trade/containers/zones. 8 The words.
- **Owner rulings on §25 (2026-11-05):** ★1 two shared fields + a per-stat lock on the sheet (`handLocked`) — **as proposed** · ★2/3 defaults and "own" — **as proposed** (genre silent → owner and players `as-ruled`; Story/Lair pin owner `all-any`; world, NPCs and places are the session owner's; watchers never edit) · ★4/5 override and marks — **as proposed** (override passes `changedBy` incl. `rules`, never a lock `all` or earshot; only the owner locks; rulesets lock as `['rules']`) · **★6 → rename the key** instead of inverting stored values: Honour mode (label kept) gets a new key meaning "keep every turn"; the old `honorMode` is read once (sessions + preset settings + import files + plugin presets) and mapped: old `true` → keep-turns **off** + players `as-ruled`; old `false` → keep-turns **on** + players `no-edits`; absent → genre defaults; then dropped (a second boot finds no old key — idempotent without a ledger) · **★7 → moves are their own permission:** a separate per-role control for moving items (give, drop, pick up), declared the same way as sheet latitude, so a strict table can let players hand items over while sheets stay locked (levels and name: the SDK lane proposes, ★) · ★8 words — **accepted** · ★pb-1 keep (★34) · copy accepted: ★L-1, ★L-2, ★bf-3, ★§25-8.
- **Owner ruling on ★m3b-V2-1 (2026-11-05): "There's no such thing as a pub wide book, unless it's a primer/starter" → "1. And plugins books are starters."** Supersedes the reading of ★M3-1 ("Pub wide, …", 11-05 morning) that m3-core built (a plugin's provided books readable pub-wide and attached directly to sessions; §23 seating from the attached book). Now: **a plugin's provided books are starters** — a session starts from a copy in its own book; nobody's play touches the original. Plan **§26** below; challenger first. Also ruled: **places are copied into the session's notes on first write** (as people) — lane **pl**, with the m3b-V2 scope finding 1 (the own book made before the writer check).

## 26. PROPOSED (owner ruling 2026-11-05) — a plugin's provided books are starters — challenger before lanes
**Today:** a provided book is a real lorebook row marked `provided` (`plugins/providedMarks.ts`), readable by every member (`providedBooksReadableBy`, m3-core), listed in the Lorebooks view's **Ready-made** group (m3b-F) and the start form's **Your lorebooks**, attached directly (`lorebookId`) by `sessions:create`/`sessions:update`/the applet's `create-session`; §23 seats its provided-marked cast from the attached book; pb upgrades it in place under person-wins. Primer rows (core primers, M2e) are already "started from, never read into a session" (`kitReadRefusal`, ★M3-core-2) and copied by `starterKit` → `bookFromKit` into the session's own book.
**Proposed:** (1) a provided book behaves as a primer row: offered in the book pick's **Start from a starter lorebook** group (not **Your lorebooks**), refused as a session's attached book in words (the primer's sentence), copied into the session's own book at create via the `starterKit` path; (2) the applet's `create-session` naming a provided book's id (or slug) starts from it (the launcher flow unchanged for the person); (3) §23 seating reads the **copy**: the copied book's cast members are seated (the same provided-marked cards, never duplicated — check what `bookFromKit` does with cards and bindings); (4) the Lorebooks view keeps showing it under **Ready-made**, read-only with **Clone**/**Start a session from it**; (5) pb's upgrade changes the starter only; copies already made are the sessions' own (as primers); (6) **existing sessions attached to a provided book** — ★: keep reading it (0.6 rule; no data change) or convert each into its own copy by a boot pass; (7) m3b-V2's "can't write its book" still covers members on someone's shared book; (8) Mundi's launcher Continue/Load keep working (they open sessions, not books).
**Lanes after the challenger:** core (server + client) **ps**; the port a check-only follow-up.
- **§26 challenger round 1 (2026-11-05) — five objections, all taken:** (1) a provided book can't ride the `starterKit` path as is: every starter decider keys on `kind = 'starter_kit'` (`starterKits.ts` `resolveKit` ~165–172, `kitReadRefusal` ~264–277, `starterKitCards` ~326–331; `providedMarks.ts` `primerRowEditProblem` ~176) — the mechanism is the primer-row precedent: provided rows are imported as `kind = 'starter_kit'` and a boot pass flips existing ones (second-boot test); consequences: the enabling admin no longer edits it (read-only like a primer; Clone to edit), it shows on _Your starter lorebooks_, `EditSessionForm` drops it from its list (see 3). (2) point (3) was wrong: `bookFromKit` **copies** cards per start for a provided row (`ownCastCards(…, { all: kit.provided })`, `starterKits.ts` ~497–610 — 5–10 new cards in the member's Characters list per launcher start) and §23 seating never runs on a kit start (`sessions.lorebookId` stays null; seating gates on it, `sessions.ts` ~1064; `bookCastToSeat` on the mark; the client's cast line on `lorebookId`) → ★. (3) existing sessions attached to a provided book: once it is a starter, every settings save is refused (`sessions:update` runs `kitReadRefusal` on the sent `lorebookId` before the "already reads" check, `sessions.ts` ~5918–5935; `checkLorebookOwnership` requires `kind === 'book'` ~256) and a boot pass can't copy into the own book (it exists, overlays the book, holds m3b-V2's values) → keep them reading it, exempt "the book this session already reads" from those checks (as `isNewAttachOfDisabledBook`), keep it in their edit form's list, point to the existing **Clone with changes and attach** (`ownBook.ts` `cloneWithChanges`). (4) no slug field (would be a new SDK name): the start form maps a prefilled `lorebookId` that is a kit row to `flow.starterKit`; the port stays unchanged while `library-lorebooks` lists the starter. (5) names/docs: NOMENCLATURE rows **provided book** (~3165 "Not a starter kit" — now the opposite), **starter lorebook** (~386 "three sources"), `providedBooksReadableBy` (~3203), `castFromLorebook` (~3212 "the attached lorebook"), the _Who is in it_ fact (~3207); `checkLorebookOwnership`'s comment; `docs/plugins.md` ~54; pb's person-wins protects only edits made on 0.6 data from now on; ★m3b-V2-1 closed by the ruling.
- **Owner rulings on §26 (2026-11-05):** "Plugins can only ship lorebooks as starters. **They contain cast, not cards.** If a user wants a cast member as a card, they can export the cast member as a card to their account." · seating: **"the session's book" = the attached book, else its starter copy** (for every genre that declares `castFromLorebook`, primers and admin starters included) · existing sessions attached to a plugin book: **keep reading it; offer Clone with changes** (exempt "the book it already reads" from the starter checks; keep it in its edit form's list). The "cast, not cards" ruling changes §26's shape (provided books import card-less cast members; seating must seat card-less members; an export-as-card action; the 0.6 data that already holds provided cards; the port's avatars keyed by character id) → **a second design pass before any §26 lane.**
- **pl (2026-11-05):** items 2–3 BUILT (`plans/REPORT-pl-2026-11-05.md`: `assertOwnBookWriter`; the own book's lorebook row only where read); places blocked → **★pl-1 ruled: "In the notes' overlay, like the rules' place fields"** (the existing `extra_json.ruleFed.entryFields[<book entry id>]` overlay on the notes' own row, extended to attribute values — never a copied entry, ★T3-5 stands) → lane **pl2**.
- **sl BUILT (2026-11-05; `plans/REPORT-sl-2026-11-05.md`; scope review: no objection, 3 smaller findings).** ⚠ Finding 1: the rulesets' `['rules']` marks are live since `sdk:build` — today's gate refuses the **session owner** (as `gm`) on every Vis Itineris attribute and Wherewithal's pools until lane **ed** pins Story/Lair owners to `all-any`: sl and ed ship together; no walk between them. Finding 2: the readers `sheetLatitudeOf`/`itemMoveLatitudeOf` and `HAND_EDIT_CAUSES` are exports nobody calls yet — ed uses them or they go. Finding 3: `whose`'s doc says "the value's owner" beside `role: 'owner'` (R1) → reword "Whose the value is, seen from `role`".
- **Owner rulings on sl's ★ (2026-11-05):** ★sl-1 field labels **accepted** · ★sl-2 move levels **accepted**, Crawler players' moves default **Their own items**; owner direction: "we can allow scripting to determine where items can be moved based on state when rules are enforced" — a ruleset/genre script that judges a move from state (a proposal for after ed; not built yet) · ★sl-3 a per-stat lock beats the session-wide level · **★sl-4 → rename the label too: "Keep every turn"** (key `keepEveryTurn`; the words "Honour mode" leave the UI) · **★sl-5 → both: the SDK ships a default sentence per cause, the app may override** (attribute names filled by the caller).


## 26b. DESIGN PASS (2026-11-05) — "cast, not cards" — not a go; ★ for the owner
(Plan agent, pasted by the lead.) **Today:** card-less cast members exist (`lorebook_bindings.character_id` nullable, `SET NULL`, `schema.ts` ~1550; UI "background character", `CastMemberForm.svelte` ~220–228; they hold name, aliases, summary, state, the retold columns and a `face`, `cast_member` stats, lore, presences, relationships — but the file today carries only `bindingText`/`spriteSet` for them; export omits retold columns and face, `lorebookExportBuilder.ts` ~186–197). A provided book's cards are made by `importFresh` → `restoreBoundEntities` (admin-owned) + `markBroughtCharacters`/`stampProvidedCharacter`; pb's `upgradeCastAndStats` rewrites them. Mundi's files carry 5/10 embedded cards. **A seat must be a card:** `session_characters` = a card or an envoy (unique session+card), `state.cast.byId` keyed by card id, messages name speakers by `character_id`; the card-less player seat is the one precedent; cards can't be hidden (`characters.user_id` NOT NULL, the list filters only `userId`/`isDeleted`). Starting from a provided row copies every card into the starter's library (`ownCastCards(…, { all: kit.provided })`). "The session's book" decider exists: `attachedOrOwnBookOf` (`ownBook.ts` ~235). **Export:** no card-from-member action; nearest `characters:duplicate` (**Copy to my characters**). **Port:** keys people by card id (`avatarsOf` by name → `Number(id)`; `annexAvatars`). **0.6 data:** deleting provided cards breaks seats, speakers, `session_cast` stats, the Mundi annex ids and m3b member sessions — keep as legacy.
**Proposed shape:** (1) a provided book imports card-less (embedded cards → the member's own words: name + retold text columns, retold flags on; tags/extensions/creator notes/timbre dropped, said in the sync report; `ProvidedCastMark.character = null`; pb hashes the member's columns); (2) seating reads `attachedOrOwnBookOf`; at create, inside the transaction on `tx`, `bookCastToSeat` makes one hidden 🚧 **seat card** per card-less member it seats (owned by the session's owner, linked to the copy's member, hidden from every list); the shape check counts the starter's non-persona members first; carded members seat their own card; (3) a session's copy of a provided row turns its cards into seat cards, not library copies; (4) **Copy to my characters** on the member page.
**★26b-1** seat model: (ii) a hidden seat card per seated member (rec) vs (i) seat by cast member (migration + ~50 server files + port engine/saves). **★26b-2** the file: the importer reads a provided file's embedded cards as card-less members (rec; no SDK change; a person's own Import still makes cards) vs a new SDK member carrier + packager refusal. **★26b-3** seat cards only when the genre seats (`castFromLorebook`), at create (rec); other starts keep members card-less. **★26b-4** marking/lifetime: a `characters.seat_card` boolean (generated migration, no boot pass) filtered from lists/pickers; deleted with the session once nothing seats or links it; branches seat the parent's (rec) vs an `extensions` JSON mark. **★26b-5** export: **Copy to my characters** on the member page, new verb 🚧 `lorebooks:castMemberToCard`, built from the member as read (own name, retold text, face → avatar), anyone who can read the book, to the asker's account, not linked back (rec). **★26b-6** 0.6 data: keep as legacy (rec); pb keeps two cast paths. **★26b-7** scope: provided books only (+ seat cards for their session copies); primers and admin starters keep making library cards (rec). **★26b-8** `retoldMembersForSession` reads `attachedOrOwnBookOf` so a starter-copy session's member edits reach the prompt (rec; changes a hot reader for every starter-started session).
**Lanes after rulings (merged with §26's ps):** sdk-26b (docs only: `castFromLorebook` "the session's book"; guides) · ps-S (server: providedBooks/Marks, `sockets/lorebooks.ts` cast-only option, starterKits, seatBookCast, sessions.ts, schema + migration, characters/appletLibrary filters + export verb, retold.ts, types) · ps-C (client + docs + NOMENCLATURE incl. a 🚧 **seat card** row — _session card_ is taken, R1) · mundi-26b (check-only walk). Noticed: `copyOwnBookToBranch` copies entries, not members (a branch of a starter-copy session loses its cast rows); ★pb-4 still applies.
- **Owner rulings on §26b (2026-11-05):** **★26b-1 → "Move to seating cast instead of cards. Every session has a lorebook or [own book] now. Cards seed cast, cast defaults to reading active linked card."** — a session seats **cast members**, not cards; a card is what a cast member is made from (seeds it) and, while linked, what it reads by default. This is model (i) — the larger change (migration of `session_characters`, ~50 server files, the Mundi engine/saves) — and supersedes the hidden seat-card proposal; ★26b-3/★26b-4/★26b-7 become moot as written. ★26b-2 → read a provided book's embedded cards as card-less members (rec) · ★26b-5 → **Copy to my characters**, not linked back (rec) · ★26b-6/★26b-8 not yet asked (fold into §27). → **§27: a design and migration plan for "sessions seat cast"**, challenger, then a go on lanes.
- **pl2 BUILT (2026-11-05; `plans/REPORT-pl2-2026-11-05.md`, 165/165):** a place's world value in a session that can't write its book lives in the notes' rule-fed overlay (`extra_json.ruleFed.placeValues[<book entry id>][<slot id>]`, 🚧 `placeValues`), recorded as a lore imprint so Back/Forward take it; never a copied place (★T3-5). **Owner rulings on its ★ (2026-11-05):** ★pl2-1 → **Clone with changes writes the notes' changes into the copy** (in its transaction) · ★pl2-2 → **tie the notes' book-layer rows to the turn like session values**, so Back/Forward take a person's and the world's values kept in the notes too (only for the session's own notes; the "author layers are never anchored" rule stands for real books). → a small follow-up lane **pl3** after **ed** (both touch `state/write.ts`).

## 27. DESIGN AND MIGRATION PLAN (2026-11-05) — sessions seat cast — not a go; challenger, then ★ for the owner
(Plan agent, pasted by the lead; builds on §26b's map.) Ruling ★26b-1: "Move to seating cast instead of cards. Every session has a lorebook or [own book] now. Cards seed cast, cast defaults to reading active linked card."
**Already keyed by member (book side):** scenes (`scene_characters.binding_id`), presences, relationships, amendments, `cast_member` values. **Keyed by card (session side):** the seat (`session_characters`, unique session+card, no id), `session_cast:<cardId>`, `state.cast.byId[cardId]`, speakers (`messages.character_id`; ~55 files build `character:${id}`). Transition carriers: the card↔member map (`utils/castMemberCards.ts`) and `givePlayerSeatACard` (`sessions/seatName.ts` ~245, the rekey precedent).
**A. Seat model.** `session_characters` gains `id` and `cast_member_id` (→ `lorebook_bindings`, SET NULL + `removed_name`), unique (session, member); envoys unchanged. One writer 🚧 `sessions/seats.ts`: the card's member in the attached book (dated cards incl.), else in the own book, else mint where values go (`keepsValuesInOwnBook`): attached book if the owner owns it, else the own book (mode set aside). Attached book the session can't write: seat names M; writes land on an own-book row O with new 🚧 `lorebook_bindings.overlay_of = M` (made after the writer check); reads session layer → O → M → M's active card. Player seats stay `session_personas` + `cast_member_id`. A member reads from its active linked card (name unless `own_name`; description, personality, scenario, first message, greetings, examples, face under `retold` flags; sprites/gallery/tags card-only); card-less members read their own columns.
**B. Readers** (seats; session layer `session_cast` → 🚧 `seated_member:<memberId>`; `state.cast.byId` flips key in S4; speakers via a `seated-member:<id>` stamp; run scope `currentCharacterId` unchanged = the speaker's card or null; prompt/retold reads the seat's member in whichever book — ★26b-8 dissolves; branch/next chapter remap own-book members (closes `copyOwnBookToBranch`); access seat→member→card; stored audiences rewritten; client payloads carry both; SDK additive; Mundi reads `entry.ref`/`castMemberId` with `cardId` fallback).
**C. SDK (additive, no @2):** `ParticipantRef` + 🚧 `seated-member:<id>`, `OwnerRefV1.kind` + 🚧 `seated_member`; `character:`/`session_cast` input-only ⏳ to 0.7; `CastEntry`/`SessionCharacterV1` add `ref`, `castMemberId`, `cardId`; `characterId` 0 when card-less ⏳; `player-seat:<id>` for every player seat.
**D. 0.6 data:** a generated migration (seat `id`/`cast_member_id`, `session_personas.cast_member_id`, `lorebook_bindings.overlay_of`, owner-kind CHECKs widened; index above the applied max — coordinate with lane ed) + one idempotent critical boot pass 🚧 `seat-members` after `own-books` (`tx` only): resolve/mint seats' members, link player seats, rekey `session_cast:<card>` → `seated_member:<member>` (values, configs, carries, sheets, zones, proposals), stamp lines, rewrite audiences; unexplained rows counted once and left; attic untouched (next boot normalizes); Mundi's annex untouched (port save upgrade). Tests: migration int, boot pass with overlapping card/member id ranges, second boot, attic upgrade then boot.
**E. Stages:** S1 schema + one seat writer, dual columns (M; lanes s27-D, s27-W) · S2 readers behind the compatibility layer (L; s27-P prompt/runtime, s27-S state, s27-C client, s27-B branch/creation/import/access) · S3 SDK contract (M; s27-K, s27-G core-catalog; port mundi-27a) · S4 key flip + boot-pass steps 3–5 + card-less seats + Copy to my characters (L; s27-F, s27-X; port mundi-27b save upgrade) · S5 remove card-keyed paths (S–M; app only; SDK removals at 0.7). Walks after each stage.
**F. Interplay:** §25 "own" = the player seat + character seats whose member's active card they own; a card-less member is the session owner's · m3b-V2/pl2 simpler (`overlay_of` replaces card matching; places stay on pl2's overlay) · §26 starters copy members card-less and seat them directly; `ownCastCards(…, { all: kit.provided })` stops minting library cards · Mundi: `avatarsOf` by `entry.ref`; annex v1→v2 save upgrade · Copy to my characters from the member page and the session cast panel.
**★27-1** key = cast member id (rec) vs seat id · **★27-2** new SDK names `seated-member:`/`seated_member`, old input-only to 0.7 (rec) · **★27-3** player seats always `player_seat:<seatId>` (rec) · **★27-4** card-less `characterId` = 0 ⏳ + `castMemberId` (rec) · **★27-5** guests' members minted in the own book only (rec; reverses `runLorebookBindingCheck` minting guests into the owner's book) · **★27-6** card unlinked/deleted → snapshot into the member's retold columns, seat plays on (rec) · **★27-7** speaker as a `metadata.speaker` stamp (rec) vs a new column · **★27-8** one idempotent boot pass (rec) vs a ledgered data upgrade · **★27-9** every starter copies members card-less; legacy provided cards stay (rec) · **★27-10** book swap moves seats by card, copies unmatched card-less into the notes (rec) vs refusing swaps.
- **§27 challenger round 1 (2026-11-05) — five objections, all taken:** (1) HIGH: 0.6 sessions can hold two seats that resolve to one member (a member has a linked card + dated cards; nothing refuses seating a second card of a seated member; a card can be both a character seat and a persona seat) → the unique (session, member) index and a *critical* boot pass would stop the app booting, and `session_cast` values would merge silently → a stated **merge rule** ★ + tests (two seats → one member; persona + character seat on one card). (2) HIGH: S4's flip of `state.cast.byId` keys is not additive — the Crawler preamble and the reckoning script map `character:N` → key `N` (`core-catalog …/dungeon-crawler/preamble.ts` ~187–193, `shared/reckoning.ts` ~164–170), Mundi's `avatarsOf` does `Number(id)`; card and member ids share one range, so an old reader gets the wrong person silently; no plugin host-version gate → rec: **keep carded seats keyed `<cardId>` through 0.6, key card-less seats `seated_member:<id>`** (the card-less `player_seat:<id>` precedent, `state/resolve.ts` ~1596–1616), `castMemberId` on every entry, flip at 0.7 with SDK 1.0 — or a ★ quoting "Removing or renaming a published SDK name is the owner's call" + a host floor. (3) HIGH: B/D miss stored refs — turn order in the `core` annex (`sdk/src/turnOrder.ts` ~60–71), `turnTally` (`pipelines/routines/turnTally.ts` ~44–58), `session_messages.rewind_taken` (Forward after the pass would restore dead `session_cast`/`character:` rows), speech Auto voice picks keyed by speaker ref (`speech/resolveVoice.ts` ~282; timbre resolved only for `character:`/`player-seat:` ~135–148, 263–274), persona seats' values (`session_cast:<personaCardId>` → `player_seat:<seatId>` under ★27-3); size: ~128 non-test files (app+SDK) use `character:${`/`session_cast`/`sessionCharacters` + 122 client files use `characterId` → S2/S4 closer to XL; GO per stage. (4) MEDIUM: the boot pass must not be *critical* (only the attic is; a bad row would block every boot and recovery) → one transaction per session, a no-op fast path, `failsHealth`, unexplained rows counted and left; it must live as long as the attic (the attic ETL writes `session_characters` itself) or the attic writes `cast_member_id`. (5) MEDIUM: card-less members have no voice (timbre is on the card; §26b drops it) and no sprites (sprite sets hang off cards); §26's ps lanes must wait for S4 or a Mundi start seats nobody (`bookCastToSeat` skips card-less members) → ★ voice/sprites for card-less members. Between S1 and S4 a card-less seat appears only by deleting a card (today: "departed") — S4 must tell departed from card-less by `cast_member_id`.
- **Owner rulings on §27 (2026-11-05) — GO for all stages ("Do all"):** ★27-1/2 **switch keys now** — "No one is running plugins in pre release builds yet" — and **remove the old card-based names now** (`character:<id>` refs, `session_cast` owners, `characterId` on cast entries; cast entries carry `castMemberId` + optional `cardId`, null when card-less); core genres, the reckoning script and Mundi updated in the same programme · **merge rule:** the member's active card's seat survives, the other departs, lines re-stamped to the survivor, values merge newest-wins; a persona seat always stays a persona seat · **members carry voice and sprites** (own over card, like retold text; an embedded card's voice/sprites come with it on import) · ★27-3 **every player seat keyed `player_seat:<id>`**, carded or not · ★27-5 **guests' members in the session's book, as today** · ★27-6 a card unlinked/deleted → its words copied into the member, the seat plays on · ★27-7 **a new cast-member column on messages** (speaker), not a metadata stamp · ★27-8 (challenger): one idempotent boot pass, **not critical**, one transaction per session, a no-op fast path, `failsHealth`, unexplained rows counted and left; it lives as long as the attic (or the attic writes the new columns) · ★27-9 **every starter copies people card-less** (primers, admin kits, plugin books); legacy provided cards stay linked · ★27-10 → **"All sessions have a lorebook, [own book] or overlay. No exceptions so SDK data storage API is predictable. No swapping lorebooks. But [own books] can be surfaced and overlays can have their parent merged in place."** — a session's book is fixed at start; attach/swap/stop-reading go; Share as a lorebook (surface the own book) stays; a new **merge the parent in place** for an overlay session (★ exact behaviour in its lane).
- **§27 v2 stages (lead, after the rulings):** **S1 schema + seat writer** (one generated migration: `session_characters.id` + `cast_member_id` (unique session+member), `session_personas.cast_member_id`, `lorebook_bindings.overlay_of`, a speaker cast-member column on `messages` and `session_messages`, member-owned voice and sprite-set links, owner-kind CHECKs widened for `seated_member`; `sessions/seats.ts` the one seat writer used by every seating site incl. `runLorebookBindingCheck`; the boot pass `seat-members` steps 1–2 with the merge rule; dual-write, behaviour unchanged) → **S2 readers to members** (state, prompt/runtime, speech/voices, sprites, turn order/tally/rewind, branch/next chapter, access, client) with card refs still accepted → **S3 SDK + core-catalog + Mundi switch** (`seated-member:` / `seated_member`, `castMemberId`/`cardId`, old names removed; the port's save upgrade) → **S4 the flip** (boot pass steps 3–5: rekey values/configs/carries/sheets/zones/proposals, `rewind_taken`, turn order/tally, speech picks, audiences; persona-seat values → `player_seat:<seatId>`; card-less seats; every starter card-less; Copy to my characters; no switching books + merge-parent-in-place) → **S5 remove** the card-keyed paths and the compatibility reads. §26's ps lanes fold into S4. Walks after each stage. Lane ed and pl3 finish first where files overlap (`state/**`).
- **Owner direction (2026-11-05), on the lead's user-POV review ("cards are static seeds, cast are evolving, living characters"):** the live link stays (cast reads its active linked card by default), made visible: **"We can list lorebooks a card is linked in from their profile."** Already built in part: the card editor's cast line _In 2 lorebooks:_ (`characterForms/castBannerWords.ts`, `lorebooks:bindingsForCharacter`, retold fields in its title). For S2 (★, built as recommended unless ruled otherwise): show the line on the card's **profile/view** too, and name the consequence at the point of editing — _Editing this changes Bianca in Porto Alto and The Shared House (her description and greetings; her personality is her own there)._ Other user-POV notes for S2/S4 UI: two of the owner's stories on one book share one living character (say so where it matters); a guest's character lives in the host's book (say so where they seat it; Copy to my characters takes it home); the session's cast panel and a message's name open the **cast member**, not the card editor.
- **Owner direction (2026-11-05) — the Characters view lists cast too:** "Should we change the characters sidebar to also show cast members? Filterable" → "Give each character badges: card, cast. Not mutually exclusive." Lead's reading for S4's client lane (★ details, built as recommended unless ruled): **one list, one row per character** — a card and the cast members linked to it are one row with both badges (**Card** · **Cast**, the latter with "in N lorebooks"); a card-less member is its own row (**Cast** only); a card no book holds is **Card** only. Filters by badge, by lorebook/story, "follows its card / has its own words". Members kept only in one story's notes are behind an "In my stories" filter by default (starter copies would otherwise flood the list). A member opens its page in the Lorebooks view (which owns its edits, P2); a card opens the card editor; "Copy to my characters" on a Cast-only row. The rail item likely becomes **Characters** (NOMENCLATURE: the view is not the card type). Needs one new paged, permission-filtered cast read across books.
  - **Refined (owner, 2026-11-05):** "the cast badge is probably redundant, any card that's been used will be a cast" → **one badge, Card** (the row has a seed card); every row is a character; the row's reach line says _In 2 lorebooks_ / _Not in a lorebook yet_ (an unused card) / the lorebook a card-less member lives in. Filters: has a card / no card, by lorebook or story, follows its card / has its own words.

Lead's decision: the session's own book is its lorebook, so its places take the turn like its world row and its people (one rule for every own-book row); the turn ties are dropped only when rows are copied into another book (a clone). ★pl3-3 (Back stops at Clone with changes) **not accepted** — read with "No swapping lorebooks" + "overlays can have their parent merged in place": Clone with changes (which re-points a session to another book) is a swap and goes in S4, replaced by **merging the parent into the overlay in place**, where the session's values keep their turn ties. Confirmed: a person's notes value follows that character's own turn (R9).

## 29. STAGE S3 PLAN (2026-11-05) — SDK grammar, consumer switch, core gates (seat-cast programme) — Plan agent, pasted by the lead; challenger next
**Lead's staging note:** the v2 stages bullet put "old names removed" in S3, which would remove names core still emits until S4. The plan proposes removal in **S5**; because that moves the timing of the owner's ruling ("remove the old card-based names now", 2026-11-05), it is **★S3-7, an ask** (challenger objection 5).
## S3 plan: SDK grammar, consumer switch, core gates (seat-cast programme)

### The ordering rule that settles the constraint
S3 **adds** names. It moves every consumer onto **identity fields the host fills in**, so consumers stop building refs or keys from card ids. Core keeps emitting the card form until S4. In S4 core flips the *values* in those fields, and consumers need no change. In S5 the SDK names are removed together with the app's compatibility reads.

This means the v2 stages bullet's "S3 … old names removed" (PLAN line 536) is re-sequenced to S5. It stays inside the ruling: "remove now" means within this programme, not deprecate-to-0.7. The SDK grammar has to keep parsing `character:` through S4, because S4's door translates legacy input in the other direction.

### What S3 adds to the SDK (no `@2`, no package bump; `@1` ids keep evolving in place, per memory "No @2 before 0.7")
- `sdk/src/participants.ts:83-96`: `ParticipantRef` and `ParsedParticipantRef` gain 🚧 `seated-member:${string}`.
  - `parseParticipantRef` and `formatParticipantRef` (`:246-298`) learn the new form, and the error sentences (`:256-258`, `:279`) name it.
  - `character:` is marked ⏳ "removed in S5".
- `sdk/src/widgets.ts`:
  - `OwnerRefV1.kind` (`:1071`) gains 🚧 `'seated_member'`.
  - `SessionCharacterV1` (`:1126-1133`): `ref` widens to `ParticipantRef`, and the entry adds `castMemberId: number | null` and `cardId: number | null`. `characterId` is marked ⏳.
- `sdk/src/turnLock.ts:68` `TurnOwner.kind` gains `'seated_member'`.
- `SessionCastV1` rows (`participants.ts:385-430`): `sessionCharacters[]` and `sessionPersonas[]` gain host-filled `ref`, `castMemberId` and `cardId`.
- `contracts/src/queries.ts:2870-2890` (`session-state@1` prose): each `cast.byId` entry carries `ref`, `castMemberId` and `cardId`. The translated kinds at `:3040` add `seated_member`.
- Docs: `INTEGRATING.md` §1c (~881), §1d and §1f (~1050) list `seated-member:<id>`.

### What core changes in S3 (still no stored key and no emitted ref flips)
- **The `isParticipantRef` gates** start accepting `seated-member:` as soon as the grammar does. Behind each gate, translate through the one door `state/seatRefs.ts` `storedSeatRef`, so stored data stays uniform:
  - `host.ts:3442`: the AI-view speaker, before `aiHolds` (`participants.ts:165` compares canonical strings, so a member ref would never match a stored `character:` audience).
  - `host.ts:7189`: `namedRef`, which is stored as `metadata.speaker`.
  - `host.ts:9882`: the form addressee.
  - `sockets/sessions.ts:10123`: fireTurn, plus `sameEntry`.
  - `sockets/pipelines.ts:2827/2839`: `portrayalLinesOf`, which gets a `seated-member` branch for names.
- **Stored data-audience writes** go through the same door. `dataAudienceFindings` (`participants.ts:218`) will now accept member refs, and without the door S3 would store mixed audiences.
- **Every `parsed.kind === "character"` site** must translate first or handle the new kind: `portrayals.ts`, `fireAction.ts`, `bindings.ts`, `speakerRotation.ts`, `runTurn.ts`, `coreTools.ts`, `adventureContext.ts`, `keyProposal.ts`, `resolveVoice.ts`, `jump.ts`, `actingPerson.ts`, client `castTag.ts` (found by grep).
- **The configure-stat and retire halt sentences** (`host.ts:8539`, `:8609`) add `seated-member:<id>`.
- **Host objects carry the new fields**:
  - `state/resolve.ts:1597-1640` entries get `ref`, `castMemberId` (S2's `seatMemberId`) and `cardId`. The card-less player seat gets `ref: player-seat:<id>`, `cardId: null`.
  - The `session-cast` rows.
  - `client/.../projections/characters.ts:354`.
  - `sockets/state.ts:608` owner rows (★S3-2).
  - `sprites/choices.ts:74` (★S3-4).

### Consumers switched in S3 so they work under both S3 and S4 data
- **core-catalog scripts** stop building refs and parsing keys:
  - `seatRefOf` (`dungeon-crawler/preamble.ts:186`), `refOf` (`roundScript.ts:83`), and the `"character:" + ch.id` sites (`preamble.ts:294-295`, `shared/reckoning.ts:179/185`, `lair/scripts.ts:83/89`, `story.ts:193`) become `seat.ref`.
  - `stateKeyOf` (`preamble.ts:187-193`, `reckoning.ts:164-170`) becomes a lookup: the `state.cast.byId` key whose entry's `ref` equals the ref. It works whatever the key is.
  - `story/reckon.ts:173` (a message's `characterId`) needs ★S3-3.
- **core-catalog widgets**:
  - `stateView.ts:480/532`, `portraits.ts:294` and `places/index.ts:155` accept `session_cast | seated_member`.
  - `portraits.ts:347` matches by `ref` or `castMemberId`, not `o.id === characterId`.
- **Mundi (read side)**:
  - One helper reads the host fields in place of `Number(id)` and template refs: `genre/newGame.ts:94-99`, `world/deriveBook.ts:300-306`, `engine/save.ts:173/186/249/310/355`, `relationships.ts:338/353`, `pipelines/turns.ts:106/127/137`, `phone.ts:205`, `talkActions.ts:139`, `diary.ts:71`, `judge.ts:145`.
  - The `annexAvatars` values (`engine/save.ts:41`) are read as v1 card ids and resolved through each entry's `cardId`.
  - A v2 reader (member ids, through `castMemberId`) ships now, with no writer yet.

### Save upgrade (annex v1→v2) → S4, as mundi-27b
- **Why not S3:** v1 card ids still resolve in S3, so nothing needs v2 yet.
- **Why S4:** card-less seats, the first case v1 can't express, appear only in S4.
- **Back/Forward:** they restore v1 annexes, so the reader stays dual until 0.7.
- **Branch risk:** `sessions/branch.ts:145` copies annexes verbatim, and S4's branch remap mints new own-book member ids that core can't rewrite inside a plugin annex. Mundi must re-resolve an unknown id by its avatar tag (`extensions.mundi.avatar`).

### Removal timing
- **S4:** core flips every `ref`/key value and stops emitting the card forms. The boot pass also needs to rekey `metadata.speaker` (`host.ts:7180-7191`), form-block `addressee`/`by` (`messageBlocks.ts:458/544/601`) and any audience stored during the S3 window. These are not on today's S4 list.
- **S5, one SDK change:** drop the `character:` kind, `session_cast` from `OwnerRefV1` and `TurnOwner`, and `SessionCharacterV1.characterId`. Narrow `ref` to `seated-member:` / `player-seat:` / `envoy:`. Drop the app's door translation.
- **After S5:** old receipts' `character:` portrayal keys are filtered out silently by `portrayalLinesOf`. I'd accept that.

### Golden tests and sdk:build sequencing
1. **K** (SDK types) → `sdk:build` #1.
2. **C, G and mundi-27a** run in parallel.
3. → `sdk:build` #2, for core-catalog's dist.
4. → `npm run sdk:test` and the app tests. Repin with dated comments:
   - `sdk-tests/coreCatalog.test.ts` (golden ~258; scripts are embedded).
   - `participants.test.ts`.
   - The fixture casts in `dungeonCrawlerWalk100.test.ts` and `lair.test.ts`, which need `ref` on their rows.
   - App `pipelines/boot/specHashes.test.ts`: the Crawler, Story/reckoning and Lair specs.
   - Check `registryHashes.test.ts`.

Each build runs only after `pgrep -af "vite dev"` shows no serene-pub dev server. Mundi links the SDK through symlinks (`node_modules/@serene-pub/* -> ../../../serene-pub-sdk`), so it rebuilds and repackages after build #2. Then the walk.

- **s27-K (SDK)**, 1 worker: `sdk/src/participants.ts`, `widgets.ts`, `turnLock.ts`, `shapes.ts` (sprite-choices doc), `contracts/src/queries.ts`, `INTEGRATING.md`, `sdk-tests/participants.test.ts`.
- **s27-C (core)**, 2 workers:
  - C1: `pipelines/runtime/host.ts` gate and halt hunks, `bindings.ts`, `portrayals.ts`, `fireAction.ts`, `speakerRotation.ts`, `runTurn.ts`, `tools/coreTools.ts`, `prompt/adventureContext.ts`, `ranking/keyProposal.ts`, `speech/resolveVoice.ts`.
  - C2: `sockets/sessions.ts` (fireTurn hunk), `sockets/pipelines.ts`, `sockets/state.ts`, `sockets/jump.ts`, `state/resolve.ts`, `sprites/choices.ts`, `auth/actingPerson.ts`, the session-cast query, the audience write door, client `projections/characters.ts` and `castTag.ts`, NOMENCLATURE §25.
- **s27-G (core-catalog)**, 2 workers:
  - G1: the scripts (`dungeon-crawler/*`, `shared/reckoning.ts`, `lair/scripts.ts`, `story/reckon.ts`).
  - G2: the widgets, plus the sdk-tests repins.
- **mundi-27a**, 1 worker: the files listed above.

This **replaces the lead's S2 rule "a seat reads the card it holds"** (`overCards`, taken after the S2 challenger to keep today's output) — prompt, greetings, names, faces, voice, sprites, ownership ("own" = the active card's owner, as §25 ★2/3 already said), "can act", fire-turn, access (viewing follows the member's active card), lists and payloads. Built in lane **s27-S2b** (item 0). Tests that pinned "a seat holding a dated card reads that card" change with this ruling, quoted.
- **Owner ruling (2026-11-05, late): "If a casts feature in a lorebook/session can't survive without a card, then that feature needs to be copied to the cast member."** Generalises ★27-6 (a card's words copied into the member on unlink/delete) and the member voice/sprites ruling: every card-held feature a session or lorebook uses for a cast member gets a member-side home, filled when the member is made card-less (starter copies ★27-9, unlink, delete, Copy-from-provided) — so a card-less member plays exactly as its carded self did. **S4 scope**; a read-only sweep lists the features first (`plans/briefs/INVENTORY-s27-S4-member-parity-2026-11-05.md`).
- **S4 member-parity inventory (2026-11-05):** `plans/briefs/INVENTORY-s27-S4-member-parity-2026-11-05.md`. **No member home yet** (S4 adds one each, filled whenever a member becomes card-less): post-history instructions, group-only greetings, strokes (paused), creator notes, likeness (prompt, negative, reference image; portrait and sprite-fill actions), gallery (members have only book uploads), card embedding for retrieval; persona description on a card-less player seat isn't wired to the member's description. **Homes with gaps:** member sprite sets (no writer; a book duplicate doesn't copy them), member voice (no writer), member face (a duplicate copies the pointer, not the file). **Identity keyed by card** (the flip's list): `session_cast` owner, `cast.byId`, `character:` turn and tie refs, the private-lore gate for character speakers, entity recognition (`loadVocabulary` skips card-less members), clone-with-changes matching, Mundi phone lanes/presence/avatars. **Copy paths that drop features today:** unlink copies nothing; starter copy (`ownCastCards`) misses tags, sprite sets, gallery, card stats and the likeness file; book duplicate misses member sprite sets and re-owning the face file; `copyOwnBookToBranch` copies no members; card duplicate misses sprites, gallery, dowry. Library-only (no session reader, no member home needed): tags, category, creator, source, version, favourites, folders, character book.
- **Owner direction (2026-11-05, late) — one character profile, versioned two ways (design pass pending, not a go):** the card's fields move into one profile table owned by a card or a cast member ("make the characters table fields a separate table … managing the fields in one place programmatically"); cast members get every card feature (gallery and all). **No separate dated per-field change mechanism** (owner: "I'm skeptical of keeping with the dates per field changes as a separate storage mechanism") — cast amendments become profile versions. **"Cards can get versions but they would need to be handled differently. I.e. someone can create an explicit version 2 of the same card. Where cast is more of evolution and branching across time and branching."** Card versions = explicit authored releases (labelled); cast versions = evolution along the timeline and across branches (start moment, branch, writing turn for Back/Forward), parented on a specific card version. Open ★: (1) a card's new version — following members stay pinned with an _Update ‹member› to card v2_ offer (rec) vs move automatically; (2) in-place card edits keep reaching members following that version (rec, today's live link); (3) may a story event write a cast version; (4) this as S4's first stage with its own design pass + challenger (rec).

## 30. CHARACTER VERSIONS DESIGN (2026-11-05, late) — S4 stage 1 — Plan agent, pasted by the lead; challenger next; not a go

## Character versions: design for S4 stage 1 (read-only; not a go)

I edited nothing. Every claim below cites the code as it stands today.

### Rulings this design follows (owner, 2026-11-05, quoted as given)
- 🚧 **character version**, table `character_versions`. A **card version** is "an explicit version 2 of the same card", an authored release. A **cast version** is "evolution and branching across time and branching", and its parent is a specific card version.
- "No separate dated per-field change mechanism": cast amendments become cast versions.
- A card's new version: "members stay on their version with an _Update ‹member› to card v2_ offer". In-place card edits reach members that follow that version.
- Story events: "Yes, anchored to a turn. Expose API in the SDK as well for cast management. Edits apply to the same iteration until the time moves or a new turn starts (how the genre tracks forwards, backwards)."
- "a session branch forks a line". "Everything in session is cast -> active card." "If a casts feature … can't survive without a card, then that feature needs to be copied to the cast member."

### 1. Tables

**`character_versions`** (new; one generated migration, index above the applied max, which is 0145 today)
- **Ownership.** `id` identity. Exactly one of `card_id` (→ `characters`, cascade) and `cast_member_id` (→ `lorebook_bindings`, cascade), enforced by a CHECK. This copies the `sprite_sets_one_owner_check` precedent (schema.ts:2086-2089).
- **Card versions.** `number` (unique per card: 1, 2, …) and `label` (free text; takes over `characters.character_version`, schema.ts:2272). A card's **current version** is its highest number. No pointer column, so there is nothing a second writer could leave out of step.
- **Cast version parent.** `parent_version_id` (→ a card version, `SET NULL` as a backstop). It replaces `characterId` as the field an amendment could set, so a dated card becomes a cast version whose parent is a version of another card.
- **Where a cast version sits** (the same columns `cast_amendments` and `attribute_values` already use):
  - `lorebook_id` (copied from the member, so one query reads a whole book) and `branch_id` (→ `lorebook_branches`, cascade, null = main).
  - The moment: `year`, `month`, `day`, `hour`, `minute`. Null means the member's base version.
  - `personal_position`, kept from cast_amendments (schema.ts:9160) so that time travel still works.
  - `history_entry_id` (`SET NULL`).
  - `valid_from_message_id` (→ `messages`, cascade): the **turn anchor**. Null means authored.
  - `source_session_id` (a plain int), `written_by` (`user` · `run:<id>` · `script:<id>` · `plugin:<id>`), and `note`.
- **Field columns.** Every field a card has today, as typed columns: name, nickname, description, personality, scenario, first_message, alternate_greetings, example_dialogues (string[]; a member's text is split with `splitExampleDialogues`), group_only_greetings, post_history_instructions, strokes, creator_notes(+multilingual), `face_media_id` (the card's avatar), `timbre` jsonb (language and silent included), `likeness` jsonb (lifted out of `extensions.serenepub`), and `extensions` (the remaining keys a session or an export reads).
- **Member-only fields.** These were amendable before, so they move too (`CAST_AMENDABLE`, sockets/amendments.ts:382-402): aliases, summary, node_state, node_visibility, sprite_set.
- **How each field reads.** `set_fields text[]` and `follows text[]`:
  - in `follows`: read from the parent;
  - in `set_fields` and not in `follows`: the version's own value, which may be deliberately empty;
  - in neither: carried from the version before it (★CV-1).
  - A card version sets every field and follows none (CHECK: `follows = '{}'` when `card_id` is set).
- **Indexes.**
  - unique (card_id, number);
  - unique partial (cast_member_id) for the base version: branch, moment, personal position and anchor all null;
  - (cast_member_id, branch_id, year, month, day);
  - partial indexes on `valid_from_message_id`, `history_entry_id`, `branch_id`, `parent_version_id`;
  - `lorebook_id`.
- **Embedding.** It moves to card versions. `characters.embed_text_hash` is generated from columns that are about to freeze (schema.ts:2397-2401).

**Other schema changes**
- `lorebook_branches` gains `fork_message_id`, the name `clockMoves.ts:380` and `ownBook.ts:625` already use for this.
- `files` gains `cast_member_id`, a plain int for provenance like `character_id` (schema.ts:3448), so members get galleries.

**Gallery, sprites and voice go with the owner, not the version.**
- Gallery and sprite sets hang off the **card or the cast member**. `sprite_sets` already allows either owner (schema.ts:2056-2091).
- Why: they are asset libraries that grow across releases, and "a card shared by two books shows the same art in both" (schema.ts:2043-2048). Versioning them would copy files with every release.
- A version **chooses** from its owner's assets: `face_media_id` and `sprite_set`, the latter by name.
- **Voice** (`timbre`) is a version field. It is small and it changes as a person does. Today it already sits on both the card (extensions) and the member (schema.ts:1703).

**How a reading picks the version**
1. Take the versions on the reading's line chain (`onLineSql`, the same rule as `castOverlaysFor`, entriesOnReading.ts:178-208).
2. Drop dated rows past the moment or past their step's fork cut. On an ancestor step that has a `fork_message_id`, also drop versions anchored after it.
3. Filter by appearance (`amendmentsForAppearance`, shared/lorebooks/amendments.ts:190-199).
4. Apply in `amendmentsOnLine` order (lineReading.ts:328-345): main first, then each line in turn; within a step, by moment, then anchor, then id.
5. Fold `set_fields` into one result. The parent card version is carried like any other field.
6. Lay that result over the parent card version. For an `overlay_of` row (schema.ts:1692), lay it over the overlaid member as read instead.

The rule lives in one pure function, 🚧 `castVersionAt`.

### 2. Coalescing: what "the same iteration" means in the code
A session-originated write W (a run, a script, the SDK, or a person editing inside the session) to member M:
- **Line.** The session's line in M's book (`sessionReadingOf`, state/reading.ts:232). Own-book members are on main.
- **Moment.** The session's story clock, falling back to the line's present (y/m/d/h/min). `history_entry_id` comes from `writeDatingAt` (state/write.ts:400-418).
- **Anchor.** `anchorFor` (write.ts:597-623). The turn owner is `seated_member:M` when M is seated, otherwise the world. That is the SDK's `openAnchorFor` / `isAnchorOpen` (turnLock.ts:90-130): M's latest line, or the newest turn as a whole for the world (the Lair's R8 rule). A sealed anchor is refused in the same words as state writes.
- **The iteration** is the key (M, line, anchor, moment). If the version in force on the reading has that key and `source_session_id` = S, W updates it in place, merging `set_fields`. Otherwise W inserts a new version with that key.
- So a new turn (the anchor moves) or a clock move (the moment changes) opens a new version, and Back steps backwards through them.
- **Back** deletes the versions anchored to the message and records them in `RetractedState.castVersions` (write.ts:858-877; `rewind_taken`, rewind.ts:603-647). **Forward** puts them back, ids and all (`restoreRetractedState`, write.ts:978). A swipe takes them back the same way.
- **Where W lands.** `loreWriteRoute` decides. For `own-book` (Review, Off, or a book the session can't write), W goes to M's overlay row in the session's own book.

### 3. How `memberAsRead` reads it (one round)
The query count stays fixed however many members are asked for. Today there is one `castOverlaysFor` call per book (memberAsRead.ts:370-374).
1. Members, plus the session's own-book overlay rows for them: one query.
2. Versions for all of those members on each book's line: one query, with an OR across at most two books.
3. Parent card versions joined to `characters` (for `is_deleted` and the owner): one query.
4. Sprite sets: one query.

`MemberAsRead` gains `castVersionId`, `cardVersion {id, number, label}`, and `behindCard {to}` (which drives the Update offer). It also carries every new field: post-history, group-only greetings, strokes, likeness, creator notes. Tags drop out, because nothing in a session reads them (inventory F). `layerMemberAsRead` (memberAsRead.ts:160-267) becomes the fold described in §1.

### 4. Migration and the boot pass
- **The migration** creates the table and the columns. It drops nothing.
- **The boot pass** is 🚧 `character-versions`, in startup/index.ts after `attic` and before `own-books` / `seat-members` (seat-members is at :278). It is not critical; it runs one transaction per book and per card set, has a fast no-op path, and is marked `failsHealth`. It uses `tx` only.
- **Cards** get version 1, a straight copy. The label is `character_version`, else "1.0". Timbre and likeness are lifted out of `extensions`.
- **Each member** gets a base version:
  - own values from its columns for retold fields, `own_name`/`own_nickname`, a non-null `timbre`, the face (when `retold.face`), aliases, summary, state, visibility and sprite set;
  - every other card field goes in `follows`;
  - group-only greetings are an own `[]` when greetings are retold, which keeps what `memberAsRead.ts:202-205` does today;
  - the parent is the linked card's version 1; a card-less member has no parent and every field is its own.
- **Each cast amendment** becomes one dated version: same line, date, personal position and history entry.
  - `characterId` becomes `parent_version_id`.
  - `retold` is applied as a whole object (sockets/amendments.ts:393-394), so each flag that turns on becomes an own value: the member's column as resolved up to that amendment.
- **Idempotence.** The base version is the marker, so the second boot changes nothing. Tests: the pass, a second boot, overlapping card and member ids, every retold and amendment shape (reads before and after must be identical), and attic upgrade followed by boot.
- **The attic** keeps writing `characters` unchanged (attic/etl/characters.ts:29-135). The pass lifts those rows on the same boot.

### 5. What retires (the columns freeze ⏳ in this stage; the drop is ★CV-9)
- `lorebook_bindings`: retold, description, personality, scenario, first_message, alternate_greetings, example_dialogues, face_media_id, timbre, own_name, own_nickname, sprite_set.
- `cast_amendments` (writer: sockets/amendments.ts:888; duplicated by tableRegistry.ts:644).
- The card-field columns on `characters`.
- `retoldMembersForSession` / `RetoldMember`; the dated-card map's storage (`castMemberCards` reads parents instead).
- The member gap in `copyOwnBookToBranch` (ownBook.ts:612-712).
- What stays:
  - `characters.name`, `nickname` and `avatar_media_id`: ⏳ copies of the current version, written only by the versions module, because about 50 readers use them;
  - `lorebook_bindings.name`, which is already a synced copy;
  - node_state, kept as a copy of the main-head value for graph readers until stage 2.
- A test fails if any code other than the attic or the pass touches a frozen column.

### 6. Card editor and the Update offer
- **Save** updates the current version in place (charactersUpdate, sockets/characters.ts:606-832, moves to 🚧 `characters/versions.ts`). Members following that version change with it, which is today's live link.
- **Save as new version** inserts number + 1 with the edits. The label field (CharacterForm.svelte:1625) gets a suggested next label (★CV-4), editable. The editor then says _3 cast members stay on v1.0_ and offers **Update**.
- **Update ‹member› to card v2** shows on the member page, the card profile's `CastLine`, and the Characters row.
  - It moves that member's versions on the line from an older version of the same card to v2. A version whose parent is another card stays where it is.
  - It is authoring, not anchored to a turn. Undo is the same action back to v1.
- **Import and export.** Export writes the current version (`characterCardParser.ts:1214` label). Import creates version 1. An overwrite import adds a new version (★CV-5).

### 7. SDK cast-management API (additive; ★CV-6 for the names)
- 🚧 `core:query/cast-member@1`
  - in: a member ref (`seated-member:` or `player-seat:`) or `castMemberId`;
  - out: `CastMemberV1`, the member as read on the session's reading, including `castVersionId`, `cardVersion`, `follows`, `cardless` and `behindCard`. Optionally `versions[]` (id, moment, anchor, writtenBy, setFields).
- 🚧 `core:outlet/update-cast-member@1`
  - in: `member` and `changes` (`{ field: value | { follow: true } }`);
  - out: `main` and `castVersionId`;
  - review covers `changes` (R-15). It writes or coalesces the turn-anchored version from §2.
- 🚧 `CAST_VERSION_FIELDS`: the field vocabulary.
- Governed by `writes.lore` (descriptors.ts:687) and the lore write mode. A run only writes if a genre wires the outlet, which is the P1 opt-in.
- `update-character@1` (contracts outlets.ts:2062-2080) keeps its meaning: it writes the card's current version.

### 8. Session branches fork a line
- `branchSession` today copies the parent's line (branch.ts:117-121).
- **New:** for an attached book the branch can write, make a `lorebook_branches` row forked at the parent's clock with `fork_message_id`, and set the new session onto it.
- **Pre-fork versions:**
  - Authored versions are shared through the line.
  - The parent session's own turn-anchored versions up to the fork are copied onto the new line, re-anchored to the copied messages (as `copyStateRows` does). The step rule skips the originals, so Back in the parent can't take them from the branch.
- **Own-book members:** see ★CV-7.

### 9. Effect on S4's other items
- **Key flip:** independent of versions. `activeCardId` is the parent version's card.
- **Card-less seats:** the content barrier is gone. Every field lives on a version, so dropping the `seatedCastOf` filter (seatedCast.ts:48) leaves nothing to lose.
- **Starters copy members card-less** (★27-9): copy the member and its versions, turning followed fields into own values, with no parent. Card sprite sets are copied as member sets, and gallery files are re-owned with `cast_member_id`. This replaces `ownCastCards` (starterKits.ts:519).
- **Unlink or delete a card** (★27-6): followed fields become own values first.
- **Copy to my characters:** build the card and version 1 from the member as read; copy the gallery and sprite sets. It is not linked back.

### 10. Lanes (each lane at most 2 workers; four lanes are editing now, so take migration numbers and schema.ts in turn)
- **V-schema** (1 worker): schema.ts, the migration, NOMENCLATURE §25.
- **V-core** (2 workers):
  - 🚧 `characters/versions.ts`, the one writer;
  - `castVersionAt` in shared/lorebooks/lineReading.ts;
  - cast/memberAsRead.ts, state/entriesOnReading.ts, utils/castMemberCards.ts;
  - the boot pass and startup/index.ts.
- **V-writers** (2 workers): sockets/characters.ts, import.ts, appletLibrary.ts, library/writes.ts, portrait.ts, utils/personaCard.ts, plugins/providedBooks.ts, plugins/providedPrimers.ts, starterKits.ts, characters/cardIO.ts, utils/characterCardParser.ts, sprites/charx.ts.
- **V-member** (2 workers): sockets/lorebooks.ts (member save), sockets/amendments.ts, lorebooks/retold.ts, castOwnNames.ts, tableRegistry.ts, utils/forkHistory.ts, state/storyTime.ts:606, annotations, rowProjection.ts, narrativeGraph.ts.
- **V-time** (2 workers): state/write.ts (retract/restore, anchor), sessions/rewind.ts, sessions/branch.ts, lorebooks/ownBook.ts, the message store's deletes.
- **V-readers** (2 workers): the roughly 26 files that read card text directly (promptFields, contextFields, seatCards, greetings, likeness readers, ragContext, vectorizationQueue, illustrateScene, cardAction, backdrop, resolveVoice).
- **V-sdk** (1 worker): contracts, INTEGRATING.md, host bindings. It touches host.ts, which collides with S3's lane C, so it goes after C.
- **V-client** (2 workers): CharacterForm, CastMemberForm, castSave, retoldRows, memberDraft, AmendmentList (which becomes a versions list), CastLine, castBannerWords.

### 11. Risks
- **The lift must reproduce today's reads exactly.** That holds for per-line amendment overlays only under ★CV-1(B).
- **Frozen columns** are a second-writer hazard; the guard test is the mitigation.
- **Back** taking a version another person has since edited needs the "kept" rule (★CV-8).
- **Session branches changing line** is a behaviour change for entries and state values too (★CV-7).
- **Conflicts** with S3's `host.ts`/`state/**` work and with migration numbering.

### ★ for the owner (not yet ruled)
- **★CV-1 (ask: this interprets "No separate dated per-field change mechanism").**
  - (A) Each cast version is complete: per field, follow the card or its own value. The lift would need a snapshot at every breakpoint on every line. An edit to an earlier version would stop reaching later versions, which changes the 2026-09-23 rule "a branch reads … everything on main BEFORE its fork date".
  - (B) **Recommended:** the same table, but a version stores only what it sets. Unset fields are carried from the version before it. This is one table, amendments map 1:1, and every line rule stays as ruled.
- **★CV-2:** an anchored version shows read-only on the member page as _Written by ‹session› at turn N_, with **Keep as written**, which clears the anchor so Back no longer takes it (rec). Alternative: authors may edit it in place.
- **★CV-3:** an edit a person makes inside the session is anchored like a story event (rec, consistent with ★pl2-2). An edit on the member page is authoring.
- **★CV-4:** the suggested label bumps the last number in it (1.0 → 1.1, v2 → v3); if there is no number, the field is left empty.
- **★CV-5:** an overwrite import adds a new version (rec) instead of rewriting the current one.
- **★CV-6:** the names `update-cast-member@1`, `cast-member@1`, `CastMemberV1`, `CAST_VERSION_FIELDS`. Scope: read and update only (rec), with no add-member or link-card operations in this stage.
- **★CV-7 (ask).** Two parts:
  - (a) Forking a line also forks the branch's entry and state writes (rec yes).
  - (b) Own-book members: copy the members and their versions as of the fork, re-anchored (rec; the own book is copied anyway), versus sharing the parent's own book across the branch family on lines. That would be the literal reading, but it needs an ownership hand-off when a session is deleted, and every own-book reader would have to learn lines.
- **★CV-8:** under Review, write to the session's own-book overlay (rec, as `loreWriteRoute`) instead of filing a proposal.
- **★CV-9:** when to drop the frozen columns and `cast_amendments`. Rec 0.7, or S5 behind a check that the pass has run. A drop migration would run before the boot pass and lose data.
- **★CV-10:** node_state and visibility move into versions (rec). UI wording: keep _Retold here / From the card_ as the labels for own vs follows (rec).

### Critical Files for Implementation
- /home/jody/github/serene-pub/src/lib/server/db/schema.ts
- /home/jody/github/serene-pub/src/lib/server/cast/memberAsRead.ts
- /home/jody/github/serene-pub/src/lib/server/state/write.ts
- /home/jody/github/serene-pub/src/lib/server/sessions/branch.ts (with /home/jody/github/serene-pub/src/lib/server/lorebooks/ownBook.ts)
- /home/jody/github/serene-pub/src/lib/server/sockets/characters.ts
- **§30 challenger (2026-11-05) — five objections, all taken (design v2 after the owner's ★):** (1) HIGH the iteration key breaks: a session's reading knows the day only (`state/reading.ts` ~106–117, ~236–240), so a minute-dated version doesn't read back until the next day (Mundi on every write) → day-granular moment, a clock move (`clockMoves.ts` ~385–390) is the time boundary; a null anchor (`openAnchorFor` before anyone spoke) collides with "authored" and the base version's unique key → session writes marked by `source_session_id`/`written_by`, never a null anchor; a seated member's anchor is its own latest line, so "the iteration" outlives other speakers' turns → bound by the session's newest turn (the world rule, `isAnchorOpen`, which groups Crawler rounds). (2) HIGH the member creators have no lane (`utils/characterBindingSync.ts` `findOrInsertBinding` — seats, seat-members, worldScope, scenes, entries, attic restore; `availableSceneCast.ts` ~651) and the boot pass runs before `seat-members` → the one creation helper writes the base version (versions module owns it); pass after `seat-members`; a reader's rule for a missing version; `retold` flags turning OFF become `follows`; V-core/V-time/V-readers wait for S3 lane C. (3) MEDIUM-HIGH the `fork_message_id` step cut drops other sessions' versions by write order (the 2026-09-23 ruling cuts by story date) and forks `lineReading`'s one rule → cut only the parent session's own rows, in `Line`/`lineSql`/conformance, or drop it — folded into ★CV-7. (4) MEDIUM ★CV-1 restated as A/B/C (below). (5) MEDIUM `characters.name`/`nickname`/`avatar_media_id` as copies of the CURRENT version mislead pinned members on 31 session-path readers → route session readers through `memberAsRead`, guard test; library screens may keep the copies.
- **Owner rulings on §30 (2026-11-05, late):** **★CV-1 → C, complete versions, a read picks ONE row** (the latest by story date on the line, nearer line wins a tie, cut at the fork date; fields that follow the card follow its version; fixing an old version doesn't flow into later ones). **★CV-7 → "Fork what gets changed, when it gets changed."** — a branch shares the parent's people until one of them changes on the branch; that member (and only it) forks at that change (copy-on-write), rather than copying every own-book member at the fork or sharing one own book forever. **★CV-2 → a story-written version is editable in place** by the author (Back still takes it with its turn). **★CV-3 → an in-session edit is a story event, tied to the turn.**
