## 31. CHARACTER VERSIONS DESIGN v2 (2026-11-05, late) — after the §30 challenger and the owner's rulings — Plan agent, pasted by the lead; second challenger next; not a go

# Character versions: design v2 (S4 stage 1)

This is a read-only design and not a go. I edited nothing. Cites are to the code as it stands on 2026-11-05 (late). v1 is PLAN §30 (lines 679–863). The challenger and both batches of owner rulings are lines 864–867.

## 0. What v2 changes from v1
- **Reads.** Versions are complete, and a read picks one row (★CV-1 → C). This replaces v1's per-field fold.
- **Dates.** A version's date is a day. A clock move is the time boundary, and the iteration ends with the session's newest turn. A session's rows always carry `source_session_id`.
- **Branches.** Branches are copy-on-write (★CV-7). Nothing is copied at the fork; a member forks only when it changes. A session cut inside the one line rule keeps the parent's later turns out of the branch.
- **Turn tie.** The turn tie is `ON DELETE SET NULL`, with explicit takes on Back, swipes and deletes. So the story's evolution of a shared book outlives a deleted session.
- **Creation and lift.** One member-creation helper writes the base version. The boot pass runs after `seat-members`. There is a defined read for a member with no version. A `retold` flag that turns OFF becomes `follows`.
- **Card columns.** Library copies are kept only where named. Every session-path reader goes through `memberAsRead`, with a guard test.
- **Lanes.** The core lanes wait for S3 lane C.

## 1. Tables
**`character_versions`** — one generated migration, numbered above the applied max (0145 today).
- **Owner.** Exactly one of `card_id` (→ `characters`, cascade) and `cast_member_id` (→ `lorebook_bindings`, cascade), enforced by a CHECK. Precedent: `sprite_sets_one_owner_check`, schema.ts:2086-2089.
- **Card versions.** `number` (unique per card) and `label`, which takes over `characters.character_version` (schema.ts:2272). The current version is the highest number.
- **Cast versions.** `parent_version_id` (→ a card version, `SET NULL` as a backstop). Null means card-less.
- **Where.** `lorebook_id` (copied from the member) and `branch_id` (→ `lorebook_branches`, cascade; null = main).
- **Date.** `year`, `month`, `day`, at day grain — the grain a reading has (`momentOf` drops the time of day, state/reading.ts:102-117). A null year means undated. `history_entry_id` (`SET NULL`) is the entry the version hangs from.
  - Citation fix: `cast_amendments` carries both date columns and the entry (schema.ts:9144-9176). `attribute_values` dates only through `history_entry_id` (schema.ts:9914).
- **`at_hour`, `at_minute`.** The session clock's time at the write. Only the coalescing key reads them; a reading never does.
- **`personal_position`** (schema.ts:9160).
- **Provenance.**
  - `source_session_id` and `source_message_id`: plain ints, as in `attribute_values` (schema.ts:9927-9928).
  - `written_by` (`user:<id>` · `run:<id>` · `script:<id>` · `plugin:<id>` · `lift`) and `note`.
  - `lifted_from_amendment_id`: a plain int.
- **`valid_from_message_id`** (→ `messages`, **SET NULL**): the turn tie that Back uses.
- **Field columns.** name, nickname, description, personality, scenario, first_message, alternate_greetings, example_dialogues (text[]), group_only_greetings, post_history_instructions, strokes, creator_notes (and multilingual), face_media_id, timbre (jsonb), likeness (jsonb, lifted out of `extensions.serenepub`), extensions (the remaining keys).
- **Member-only fields.** aliases, summary, node_state, node_visibility (★CV-10), sprite_set.
- **`follows text[]`.** Fields listed here read from the parent card version. Every other field is the version's own value, which may be empty. Nothing is carried from an earlier version.
- **CHECKs.**
  - A card version has a number, `follows = '{}'`, and no branch, date, anchor or source.
  - A cast version has no number.
  - No parent means `follows = '{}'`.
  - An anchor requires a `source_session_id`.
  - An undated authored cast version can only be on main with no personal position, which makes it the base.
- **Indexes.**
  - unique (card_id, number);
  - **a unique partial index on the base version:** (cast_member_id) where card_id, branch_id, year, personal_position and source_session_id are all null;
  - (cast_member_id, branch_id, year, month, day);
  - partial indexes on valid_from_message_id, history_entry_id, branch_id, parent_version_id, and (source_session_id, source_message_id);
  - lorebook_id.

**Other columns in the same migration**
- `messages` and `session_messages` gain `origin_session_id` and `origin_message_id` (plain ints): the session and line where a copied line's turn was played.
- `sessions.branch_ancestry integer[]`.
- `lorebook_branches.made_for_session_id` (plain int).
- `files.cast_member_id` (plain int, with schema.ts:3448 as precedent), which gives members galleries.

Gallery and sprite sets stay with their owner, the card or the member (`sprite_sets` already allows either, schema.ts:2056-2091). A version chooses `face_media_id` and `sprite_set` by name.

## 2. The one-row read rule
It lives in one pure function, 🚧 `versionInForce(rows, line, moment, appearance?)`, in shared/lorebooks/lineReading.ts beside `amendmentsOnLine` (:328-345). Its cases go in `lineReading.cases.ts`, with the SQL narrowing in state/lineSql.ts.
1. **Candidates.** The rows `rowReadsOnLine` (lineReading.ts:278-289) passes: the branch is on the chain, a dated row is at or before `earlierCut(step cut, moment)`, an undated row always reads, and the session cut applies (§4a).
2. **Appearance.** Filter by `amendmentsForAppearance` (shared/lorebooks/amendments.ts:190-198) when an appearance is asked. Session reads pass null.
3. **Pick one.** The latest story date wins, with undated lowest (`compareDates`). On a tie, the nearer step of the chain wins, with main last. Then the higher personal position, then the higher id. Back, Forward and in-place edits keep ids.
4. **Fields.** A field reads its own value, or, when it is in `follows`, that exact parent version's value. A card-less member reads only its own values.
5. **Overlay row.** If the session has an overlay row O for member M (`overlay_of`, schema.ts:1692), the candidate from O wins, unless the candidate from M has a later date.

As ruled, fixing an older version never reaches a later one.

## 3. Coalescing — what "the same iteration" is
A write W from session S to member M can come from a run, a script, the SDK outlet, or a person's edit sent with a `sessionId`. Under ★CV-3 the last is a story event.
1. **Target (★CV-8).** M itself, when `loreWriteRoute` says `book` (ownBook.ts:344-353) or M is in S's own book. Otherwise S's overlay row O, made on demand. That covers Review, Off, a book someone else owns, and a parent session's own book.
2. **Line.** S's line in M's book. An own book is always main (ownBook.ts:671). A session branch still on its parent's line first calls `ensureSessionLine` (§4b).
3. **Date.** The session clock's day, else the line's present (`sessionReadingOf`, reading.ts:232-273), else the date `writeDatingAt` gives (reading.ts:368-391), else undated. `history_entry_id` comes from `writeDatingAt`.
4. **Anchor.** `anchorFor` (state/write.ts:601-627) with the **world** turn owner. This is the challenger's rule: the iteration is bounded by the session's newest turn (`isAnchorOpen`, sdk/src/turnLock.ts:121-131, which groups a Crawler round). It honours `messageId` and `ownAnchor` as state writes do. Before the first line the anchor is null and the source is still S, so the write never matches the base key.
5. **Key.** (target, branch, S, anchor, date, at_hour, at_minute).
   - If the version in force on S's reading has this key and its anchor is still open, W updates it in place, under a `FOR UPDATE` lock on the binding.
   - Otherwise W inserts a complete copy of the version in force, with the changes applied.
   - So any new turn, or any clock move forward or back, opens a new version.
6. **Change shape.** `{ field: value | { follow: true } }`. A follow is refused in words for a card-less member.

## 4. Branches: "Fork what gets changed, when it gets changed."
**(a) The session cut, inside the one line rule.**
- `Line` (lineReading.ts:66-75) gains `sessionCuts?: {sessionId, throughMessageId | null}[]`.
- `rowReadsOnLine` and `onLineSql` (lineSql.ts:32) drop rows written by a cut session whose `source_message_id` is past `through`. A null source message means the row was written before the session's first line, so it reads. New conformance cases cover this.
- `sessionReadingOf` fills the cut from `branch_ancestry`: for each ancestor, the highest `origin_message_id` among this session's live lines from that ancestor, in one GROUP BY. It is null when none are live.
- Back in the branch therefore lowers its cut with no write.
- Entries and state values do not pass the cut in this stage (★CV-12).

**(b) Attached-book members.** The branch shares its parent's line until its first book write.
- One helper, `ensureSessionLine`, then forks a `lorebook_branches` row from the branch's line at its moment (`made_for_session_id` = the branch) and moves the session onto it.
- The helper is called by the versions writer, by `bookWritePlace` (write.ts:404-422) and by `loreWriteTargetFor` (ownBook.ts:378-415).
- A member gets a row on that line only when it changes there.

**(c) Own-book members.**
- The branch's seats keep naming the parent's own-book members. `findSeatMembers` (sessions/seats.ts:191-198) widens to "an ancestor's own book, read through the cut".
- The first change makes the overlay row O in the branch's own book — the same mechanism as ★CV-8.
- No remap happens at the fork or at the change.

**(d) Changes in the parent.** P's Back, swipe or message delete may take a version V that a descendant C still reads (V's message is within C's cut).
- Before deleting, copy V to C's side: onto C's own line, or onto C's overlay row.
- The copy is anchored to C's line whose origin is P's message.
- When P goes Forward and restores V, C keeps reading its own copy.

**(e) Deleting P.**
- Versions in the attached book stay: the anchor goes null, and the plain ints keep the cut working.
- Before `purgeOwnBookOf` runs (sockets/sessions.ts ~2098; ownBook.ts:557-565), 🚧 `handOffOwnBookMembers` deals with every own-book member a descendant still names:
  - it makes the member whole as an overlay row in the descendant's own book: the versions the descendant reads are copied over, and `overlay_of` is cleared;
  - it moves that descendant's seats, line speakers, member-keyed state rows and sprite sets onto it. This is the remap S2-access listed as still owed (PLAN line 552).

Branches made before S4 carry no origins, so they keep today's sharing.

## 5. Read path (`memberAsRead`, one round)
Today: `membersAsRead` (cast/memberAsRead.ts:342-493) runs one `castOverlaysFor` call per book (:370-374), then cards, tags and sprite sets. The new round is four queries, however many members are asked for:
1. The members, plus their overlay rows in the session's own book.
2. All of their versions, narrowed in SQL per book: the attached book on the session's line with the cut; own books on main with the cut. The pick happens in TypeScript.
3. The parent card versions joined to `characters` (is_deleted, owner, the highest number for `behindCard`).
4. Sprite sets.

**Readings per book.** The attached book uses `sessionReadingOf`. An own book is read at main, at the session's moment.

**When a member has no version** — a straggler writer, or a member the pass couldn't lift — it reads the legacy way: `castMemberAt` (state/entriesOnReading.ts:222-235) plus `layerMemberAsRead` (:160-267) over the frozen columns. That read is logged once, and the next boot lifts the member. A card with no version reads its `characters` row as v1.

**`MemberAsRead` changes.**
- It gains `castVersionId`, `cardVersion {id, number, label}`, `behindCard`, `follows` (with ⏳ `followsCard` as an alias), post-history instructions, group-only greetings, strokes, likeness, creator notes, node state and visibility.
- Tags drop out (inventory F).

## 6. Back, Forward and deletes
- `retractStateAnchoredTo` (write.ts:702-829) takes the versions anchored to the message into a new 🚧 `RetractedState.castVersions` (write.ts:862-881). `reviveRetractedState` (:923) and `restoreRetractedState` (:982) restore them with their ids, and `rewind_taken` keeps them for Forward (rewind.ts:603-647).
- The message store's deletes — `deleteLegacyWhere`, `deleteLegacy` and `retractLoreOf` (messages/store.ts ~364-416) — reach `retractLoreAnchoredTo` (write.ts:840), which must take versions too, because a SET NULL key won't.
- **★CV-2.** On the member page a version shows _Written by ‹session› at turn N_ and can be edited in place. Its anchor and source are kept, so Back still takes it.

## 7. Migration, member creation, the boot pass, the S5 check
- **The migration** adds the table and columns and drops nothing.
- **One creation helper.** 🚧 `insertCastMember(tx, …)` in 🚧 `characters/versions.ts` writes the binding and its base version. Every insert site moves onto it:
  - `findOrInsertBinding` (utils/characterBindingSync.ts:276-309) and `resolveOrCreateBindingByName` (availableSceneCast.ts:620-660);
  - sockets/lorebooks.ts :1214, :1567, :2884, :3574;
  - narrativeGraph.ts :2597, :5305;
  - starterKits.ts:896, providedBooks.ts:1132, tableRegistry.ts:365.
  - A guard test fails on any other `insert(schema.lorebookBindings)`; the attic is exempt. Cards go through 🚧 `insertCard` the same way.
- **The boot pass** is 🚧 `character-versions` in startup/index.ts, **after `seat-members`** (:278-290), so the members that pass mints are lifted on the same boot. It is not critical, is marked `failsHealth`, runs one transaction per book and per card set, has a fast no-op path, and uses `tx` only.
  - **Cards** get v1 as a straight copy. The label is `character_version`, else "1.0". Timbre and likeness are lifted out of `extensions`.
  - **Each member** gets a base version:
    - own values: retold-ON fields, own names, a non-null timbre, the face when `retold.face`, aliases, summary, node state and visibility, and the sprite set;
    - group-only greetings become an own `[]` when greetings are retold (memberAsRead.ts:202-205);
    - everything else goes in `follows`;
    - the parent is the linked card's v1. A card-less member has no parent and every field is its own.
  - **Amendments become complete versions.**
    - The breakpoints are, per line of the book, each amendment date on that line's chain within its cuts, plus each personal position.
    - At each breakpoint the pass compares today's fold (`castAsOf` over `amendmentsOnLine`) with the one-row read of what it has lifted so far, and inserts a version on that line at that date wherever they differ.
    - A `retold` flag turning ON becomes an own value (the column as resolved at that point); turning OFF becomes `follows`.
    - `characterId` becomes the parent: that card's v1.
  - **Verify.** At every breakpoint and at the head, the old read must equal the new one. A book that differs rolls back, is counted, and keeps the legacy read.
  - **Idempotence.** A member that has a base, or a card that has a version, is skipped.
  - **Tests:** second boot changes nothing; overlapping card and member ids; every retold and amendment shape, including flags turning off and dated cards; branch chains with cuts; attic upgrade then boot. The attic keeps writing the old shape (attic/etl/characters.ts:29-135) and is lifted on the same boot.
- **★CV-9 (ruled for S5).** The S5 drop is a generated migration whose data upgrade has a `before` hook. That hook runs inside the migration's own transaction, before its SQL (db/dataUpgrades/index.ts:41-50), and it is the check:
  - It counts members without a base, cards without a version, and amendments with no lifted version.
  - At zero, the drop runs.
  - Otherwise this is an install that jumped from before S4 straight past S5 — migrations run before startup tasks, so the boot pass never ran. The hook runs the lift core there (pure functions plus raw SQL against S4's shape), then drops.
  - S5 also moves the attic onto versions.

## 8. What freezes, and the guard
- **`lorebook_bindings`:** retold, the retold text columns, face_media_id, timbre, own_name/own_nickname, sprite_set, node_state, node_visibility.
- **`cast_amendments`:** its writer is sockets/amendments.ts:888, duplicated in tableRegistry.ts:644-667.
- **The card-field columns on `characters`**, except four kept as ⏳ copies of the **current** version, written only by the versions module:
  - name, nickname and avatar_media_id, for library screens;
  - description, which `embed_text_hash` is generated from (schema.ts:2397-2401).
- **Guard test.** It fails on a read of a `characters` content column under pipelines/, sessions/, state/, speech/, sprites/, messages/, rulesets/, or the session, state, pipelines and jump sockets. Ids, owner and is_deleted are allowlisted. A grep of column references alone finds 43 server files outside the attic and tests; whole-row reads add more.
- **Also retiring:** `retoldMembersForSession` (lorebooks/retold.ts:234) and `RetoldMember`. `castMemberCards` reads versions' parents instead.

## 9. Card editor, the Update offer, import
- **Editor.** `charactersUpdate` (sockets/characters.ts:606-832) moves into the versions module. The editor gets a version picker (_v1.0 · v1.1 current_).
  - **Save** writes the chosen version in place. Only members following that version see the change.
- **Save as new version** inserts number + 1. The label field (CharacterForm.svelte:1625) is prefilled with the ★CV-4 suggestion. The editor then says _3 cast members stay on v1.0_ and offers **Update**.
- **Update ‹member› to card v2** appears on the member page, `CastLine` and the Characters row.
  - It re-parents that member's versions whose parent is an older version of the same card; versions parented on another card stay.
  - It is authoring, not tied to a turn.
  - The response lists what moved, so **Undo** moves exactly those back.
- **Import (★CV-5).** The uuid match and `hashCanonicalJson` compare already exist (sockets/characters.ts:1347-1395). The compare now runs against the current version.
  - Identical → `unchanged`.
  - Different → the conflict dialog offers two choices; overwrite-in-place goes (`overwriteCharacterFromParsedData` :1103; `charactersImportResolve` :1444-1512):
    - **Add as a new version.** The label is the file's `character_version`, or the suggestion when that repeats the current label. The dowry applies as an overwrite does today (:1124).
    - **Save as a new card** (a fresh uuid, as today).
  - A file that matches an older version says so: _This file is v1.0 of Bianca, which you have._
- **Export** writes the current version (characterCardParser.ts:1214).

## 10. SDK API (★CV-6: names and scope)
- 🚧 `core:query/cast-member@1`.
  - In: a member (`seated-member:` · `player-seat:` · `castMemberId`).
  - Out: `CastMemberV1`, the member as the session reads it: fields from `CAST_MEMBER_FIELDS`, `castMemberId`, `cardId`, `cardVersion`, `follows`, `cardless`, `behindCard`.
  - It sits beside `cast-presences@1` (contracts/src/queries.ts:1189).
- 🚧 `core:outlet/update-cast-member@1`.
  - In: `member` and `changes`. Out: `main` and `castVersionId`.
  - Review covers `changes` (R-15), as `update-lore-entry@1` does (outlets.ts:1748).
  - It writes or coalesces as in §3, and Back takes it with its turn.
- **Gate.** The genre's `writes.lore` (sdk/src/descriptors.ts ~1164-1177) and the lore write mode. Under Review or Off the write goes to the overlay row. Per P1, a run writes only when a genre wires the outlet.
- **`update-character@1`** (outlets.ts:2062-2080) is unchanged: it writes the card's current version in place.
- **Out of scope in this stage:** listing versions; add, link or unlink member; card-version operations.

## 11. How this fits S4's other items
- **Key flip:** independent. `activeCardId` becomes the in-force version's parent's card.
- **Card-less seats:** dropping the `seatedCastOf` filter (seatedCast.ts:48) loses nothing, because every field lives on a version.
- **Starters (★27-9):** copy the member and its versions with `follows` resolved to own values and no parent. Card sprite sets become member sets, and gallery files are re-owned through `files.cast_member_id`. This replaces `ownCastCards` (starterKits.ts:443-622).
- **Unlink or delete a card (★27-6):** every version of the member gets its followed fields turned into own values first.
- **Member parity (inventory):**
  - post-history instructions, group-only greetings, strokes, creator notes and likeness become version fields;
  - a persona seat's description reads its member's version (inventory A8).
- **Overlay rows:** ★CV-8's overlay rows are §27's `overlay_of`; this design is their read.
- **Other lanes:** §26's ps lanes follow V-core. mundi-27b is unaffected.

## 12. Lanes (at most 2 workers each; S3 W and G are built, C is running)
**Wave 0 (now, alongside C):**
- **V-schema** (1 worker): schema.ts, the migration (take the next number at the time), drizzle meta, NOMENCLATURE §25 rows (character / card / cast version, version label, follows, origin, session cut).
- **V-line** (1 worker, after V-schema): lineReading.ts, lineReading.cases.ts, state/lineSql.ts, lineSql.int, client lineConformance tests.

**Wave 1 (after C lands):**
- **V-core** (2 workers): characters/versions.ts (the one writer, coalescing, copy-on-write, `insertCastMember`/`insertCard`), cast/memberAsRead.ts, state/reading.ts (`sessionCutsOf`, readings per book), entriesOnReading.ts (legacy fallback), utils/castMemberCards.ts, characterBindingSync.ts, availableSceneCast.ts, the pass plus startup/index.ts, the guard tests.

**Wave 2 (after V-core; files don't overlap):**
- **V-time** (2 workers): state/write.ts (retract/restore, `ensureSessionLine` in `bookWritePlace`), sessions/rewind.ts, sessions/branch.ts (origins, ancestry), messages/store.ts, lorebooks/ownBook.ts (`ensureSessionLine`, `handOffOwnBookMembers`), the delete hunk in sockets/sessions.ts.
- **V-member** (2 workers): sockets/lorebooks.ts, sockets/amendments.ts (becomes the versions API), retold.ts, castOwnNames.ts, tableRegistry.ts, forkHistory.ts, state/storyTime.ts, annotations, rowProjection.ts, narrativeGraph.ts (graph state from versions), seats.ts `findSeatMembers`.
- **V-writers** (2 workers): sockets/characters.ts, import.ts, appletLibrary.ts, library/writes.ts, portrait.ts, cardIO.ts, characterCardParser.ts, charx.ts, personaCard.ts, providedBooks.ts, providedPrimers.ts, starterKits.ts.

**Wave 3:**
- **V-readers** (2 workers): the session-path card readers (seatCards, promptFields, contextFields, cardStrokes, greetings, illustrateScene, cardAction, ragContext, vectorizationQueue, backdrop, resolveVoice, sprites/choices).
- **V-sdk** (1 worker): contracts, INTEGRATING.md, host.ts bindings, `sdk:build` (only with no dev server running).
- **V-client** (2 workers): CharacterForm, CastMemberForm, castSave, retoldRows, memberDraft, AmendmentList → the versions list, CastLine, castBannerWords, the import dialog, docs.

## 13. Risks
- **The lift must reproduce today's reads exactly** under complete versions. Snapshots at every breakpoint can multiply rows. The verification step and the legacy-read fallback are the guard.
- **Story versions accumulate.** Every turn that changes a member adds a row. The pick happens in TypeScript after SQL narrowing. If `test:budgets` shows a cost, add a `DISTINCT ON` form to the conformance table.
- **Behaviour changes:**
  - A branch's entry and value writes leave its parent's line once it forks one.
  - Edits to the base stop reaching readings after the story's later versions (ruled; see ★CV-13).
- **The hand-off on deleting a parent** touches many tables. It is a rare path and needs its own tests.
- **Branches made before S4** have no origins, so the parent's later rows still reach them.
- **The ⏳ card-column copies** are a second-writer hazard; the guard test is the mitigation.
- **File and numbering conflicts:** with lane C (host.ts, sockets/sessions.ts, sockets/state.ts, state/resolve.ts, sprites/choices.ts), and with migration numbers.

## 14. ★ for the owner
- **★CV-4 — the suggested release label.** Rec: bump the last number in the label, keeping its form (1.0 → 1.1, 1.9 → 1.10, v2 → v3); leave it empty when there is no number. Trade-off: it assumes a minor bump.
- **★CV-6 — SDK names and scope.** Rec: the names in §10, and `CAST_MEMBER_FIELDS` rather than `CAST_VERSION_FIELDS`, because plugins manage members and versions are storage (R2/R3). Scope: read and update only. Trade-off: timeline-aware plugins can't list versions until a later additive field.
- **★CV-11 — when a branch forks its book line.** Rec: lazily, at its first book write, naming the line after the session. Alternative: at the fork, which puts a line in the book's list for every branch.
- **★CV-12 — the session cut for entries and state values.** Rec: yes, as a follow-up lane.
- **★CV-13 — editing an older version.** Rec: the member page says _2 later versions keep their own words_ and offers **Change later versions too**.
- **★CV-14 (ask) — a branch's seats name the parent's own-book members directly** (rec; this is the literal reading of "fork … when it gets changed"). Alternative: an empty link row per member at the fork, which avoids widening the seat trust rule.
- **★CV-9b — S5 when the lift doesn't reproduce today's reads exactly.** Rec: lift anyway and record a note per member. The S4 pass's mismatch count tells you beforehand whether any install has such members.

## 32. CHARACTER VERSIONS DESIGN v3 (2026-11-05, late) — after the §31 challenger and the owner's rulings — Plan agent, pasted by the lead; third challenger next; not a go

# Character versions: design v3 (S4 stage 1). Read-only, not a go.

I edited nothing and ran no tests. Cites are to the code as of 2026-11-05 (late). The earlier documents are PLAN §30 (lines 679–863) and §31/v2 (873–1121). The challenger's objections and the owner's rulings are at lines 1122–1124.

## 0. What v3 changes from v2
- **Life points (★CV-15).** A version carries a life point, stored as `personal_position`. "Personal position" is the canon word (NOMENCLATURE:367, R1), so "life point" stays as prose only. The lift checks every appearance tier at every breakpoint.
- **Lift anyway (★CV-9b) moves into S4.** Every book is lifted. A member whose reads differ gets a note, and the admin sees a count. The legacy read survives only for a straggler.
- **Lines belong to books.** "Sessions do not own lorebook branches, they ride along it." `made_for_session_id` is gone.
- **Write order.** Each row gets a write order, and each ancestor gets a write-order mark. One guard, the "kept row", fixes the challenger's leak without ever forking a child's line.
- **One shape for card and cast:** 🚧 **character makeup**, with merged greetings and notes.
- **Cut from v2:** Undo for Update, the bulk Update button, and the third import outcome (_This file is v1.0…_).

## 1. Tables
**`character_versions`** (one generated migration, above the highest applied number):

| group | columns |
|---|---|
| owner | `card_id` XOR `cast_member_id`, with a CHECK modelled on schema.ts:2086-2089 |
| card release | `number` (unique per card) and `label`, which takes over `characters.character_version` (schema.ts:2272). The current version is the highest number. |
| cast parent | `parent_version_id` (a card version, SET NULL). Null means card-less. |
| where | `lorebook_id`; `branch_id` (cascade; null = main) |
| when | `year`/`month`/`day` at day grain (`momentOf`, state/reading.ts:106-117); `history_entry_id`; `at_hour`/`at_minute` (used only by the coalescing key) |
| life point | `personal_position` (null = applies to every appearance) |
| provenance | `source_session_id` and `source_message_id` (plain ints); `written_by` (`user:`·`run:`·`script:`·`plugin:`·`lift`); `note`; `lifted_from_amendment_id` |
| turn tie | `valid_from_message_id` (→ messages, SET NULL) |
| order | **`write_order bigint`**: set from the id on insert; a copy inherits its source's |
| kept | **`kept_for_session_id`**, **`kept_from_version_id`** (plain ints; §5c) |
| content | the makeup columns (§2), plus `node_state` and `node_visibility` on cast versions only (★CV-10) |
| `follows text[]` | the makeup fields this version reads from its parent card version |

The CHECKs and indexes are as in v2 §1. The base index adds `kept_for_session_id IS NULL`, and there is a new index on (`kept_for_session_id`) WHERE NOT NULL.

**The same migration also adds:**
- `messages` and `session_messages`: `origin_session_id` and `origin_message_id` (plain ints; they point at the root play);
- `sessions.branch_ancestry jsonb`: `[{sessionId, mark}]`, nearest ancestor first;
- `sessions.line_on_first_write boolean`;
- `files.cast_member_id`.

It drops nothing.

## 2. One shape: 🚧 character makeup (`CharacterMakeupV1`, `CHARACTER_MAKEUP_FIELDS`)
Owner: **"Reconcile what we have for character cards. API should start with the same shape, can update both with what's best. We should also make sure we merge together any fields where there's an arbitrary duplicate like greeting and additional greetings."**

| field | card today | member today | SillyTavern / CHARX in → out |
|---|---|---|---|
| `name`, `nickname` (separate, as ruled) | columns | `own_name`/`own_nickname` | `name`; `nickname` |
| `aliases` | column | column | `extensions.serenepub.aliases` |
| `summary` (open) | column | column | `extensions.serenepub.summary` |
| `description`, `personality`, `scenario` | columns | retold columns | same names |
| **`greetings: {text, groupOnly?}[]`** | `first_message` + `alternate_greetings` + `group_only_greetings` | `first_message` + `alternate_greetings` | see below |
| `exampleDialogues: string[]` | column | column | `mes_example`, split on and joined with `<START>` |
| `postHistoryInstructions` | column | none (A1) | `post_history_instructions` |
| **`creatorNotes: {text, variants: {[iso639]: string}}`** | `creator_notes` + `creator_notes_multilingual` (schema.ts:2321-2324) | none (A4) | see below |
| `strokes` (paused) | column | none | `character_book` merge, as today |
| `faceMediaId` | `avatar_media_id` | `face_media_id` | the PNG / CHARX icon |
| `likeness`, `timbre` | `extensions.serenepub.*` | `timbre` column | `extensions.serenepub.*`; `sd_character_prompt` read in |
| `spriteSet` | null = the default set | `sprite_set` | `extensions.serenepub.defaultSpriteSet` (CHARX) |
| `extensions` | the remaining keys | none | passed through (`depth_prompt`, foreign keys) |

**Greetings.** The rule reproduces today's `buildCharacterFirstSessionMessage` (sessions/greetings.ts:43-60):
- A solo session uses the greetings not marked group-only, in order. The first of them is the default opener and the rest are swipes.
- A group session uses the group-only greetings when any exist, else the unmarked ones.

**Greetings import.** Unmarked greetings are `[first_mes, ...alternate_greetings]` with empties dropped. `group_only_greetings` become marked greetings. Both the spec location and `extensions.group_only_greetings` are read, as at sockets/characters.ts:950.

**Greetings export.** The first unmarked greeting becomes `first_mes`, the other unmarked ones `alternate_greetings`, and the marked ones `data.group_only_greetings`. That is the V3 spec location. Today the export writes them only under `extensions` (characterCardParser.ts:1176-1180), so it keeps a ⏳ copy there. The import puts unmarked greetings first, so a round trip is stable.

**Notes.**
- In: `text` = `creator_notes`, and `variants` = the multilingual map minus any entry identical to `text`.
- Out: both fields. Today's export drops the multilingual notes (characterCardParser.ts:1208).
- CHARX goes through the same builder.

**Summary: for the owner, not decided** ("Might need to discuss if there's a better way to handle the summary field"). Today it is read by embeddings (`bindingEmbedText`), relationship ranking and the core tools. Candidates:
- (a) keep it as a separate one-line "who they are";
- (b) a short form of description, `description: {text, short}`;
- (c) derived from description's first sentence unless set;
- (d) member-only, since it is the in-book blurb, and the card drops it.

**Not makeup.**
- These library facts stay on `characters`: tags, creator, category, source, folder, favourite, persona flag, uuid, and the character book.
- `node_state` and `node_visibility` are version-only.

## 3. The one-row read, with life points
🚧 `versionInForce(rows, line, moment, appearance)` sits in shared/lorebooks/lineReading.ts beside `amendmentsOnLine` (:328-345), with SQL narrowing in state/lineSql.ts:32.
1. **Candidates.** A row is a candidate when:
   - `rowReadsOnLine` passes it (:278-289);
   - the session cut passes it (§5b);
   - `kept_for_session_id` is null, or is the reader or one of its ancestors.
2. **Appearance.** `amendmentsForAppearance` (shared/lorebooks/amendments.ts:190-198) keeps rows whose `personal_position` is null or ≤ p. Session reads pass null, which means every row, as `castMemberAt` does today (state/entriesOnReading.ts:210-235). The roster passes each appearance (sockets/amendments.ts:298-313).
3. **Pick.** Latest date wins (undated lowest). On a tie: the nearer holder (the session's own overlay, then each ancestor's overlay, then the line steps nearest first); then the higher life point; then the higher `write_order`.
4. **Fields.** A field reads its own value, or the exact parent card version's value when it is in `follows`.

★CV-15 for story writes: a story write keeps the life point of the version it copies. Sessions read at null appearance, so they copy the top tier, and an earlier appearance never reads later words.

## 4. Coalescing
Most of this is unchanged from v2 §3: the target goes through `loreWriteRoute` (lorebooks/ownBook.ts:344-353), the line, the date, and the change shape. The deltas:
- **Lock.** The versions writer opens with `pg_advisory_xact_lock('stateVersion', sessionId)` (`lockStateVersion`, state/write.ts:1819), exported for it. `branchSession` takes the same lock on the parent before reading the mark, so no in-flight write can straddle a fork.
- **Anchor.** `anchorFor` (write.ts:601-627) returns null for real-book `cast_member` rows (:612-614). It gains a story-write case in V-core that uses the **world** turn owner (`isAnchorOpen`, sdk/src/turnLock.ts:121).
- **Key.** (target, branch, S, anchor, date, at_hour, at_minute, life point).
  - If the in-force row matches the key and its anchor is open, the write updates it in place, through the shared-row guard (§5c).
  - Otherwise it inserts a complete copy, keeping the life point.

## 5. Branches
Owner: **"Branch on first write. Data wise, the session is a patron of the lorebook and branch, it doesn't own it. The exception would be deleting a session who's lorebook was never surfaced as a real one"**, and **"Fork what gets changed, when it gets changed."**

**(a) A line at the first book write.**
- `branchSession` keeps the parent's line (sessions/branch.ts:121) and sets `line_on_first_write`.
- 🚧 `ensureSessionLine` lives in a new lorebooks/sessionLine.ts, owned by V-core. On the session's first write to the attached book (a version, an entry or a value), it forks a `lorebook_branches` row from the current line at the session's day, named after the session (deduped), moves the session onto it, and clears the flag.
- The line is the book's. It outlives the session, and other sessions may ride it.
- Callers: the versions writer, `bookWritePlace` (write.ts:404-422) and `loreWriteTargetFor` (ownBook.ts:378-415).
- Choosing a line by hand also clears the flag.
- Entries and values get no session cut. Patrons share the line by the book's rule. That is my reading of the ★CV-12 ruling.

**(b) The cut and its write-order mark.**
- `Line.sessionCuts` holds `{sessionId, through, mark}` per ancestor.
- A row whose source is ancestor A reads only when both hold:
  - `source_message_id` is null or ≤ `through` (`through` is the highest `origin_message_id` among the reader's live lines from A, so the branch's own Back lowers it with no write);
  - `write_order` ≤ `mark`, the highest `write_order` at the fork, taken under the lock.
- A grandchild copies its parent's entries and adds its own.
- This fixes all three leaks: coalescing at the fork message, a swipe of the fork message (same id, `retractStateAnchoredTo` is called on replacement, write.ts:662-668), and in-place edits.

**(c) The shared-row guard: 🚧 `keepForBranches(tx, versionIds)` in characters/versions.ts.**
- **When it runs.** Before any in-place change or removal of a session's row that a branch reads (the cut passes it for that branch):
  - a coalescing update;
  - a ★CV-2 edit on the member page;
  - Back, swipe or message delete;
  - a member-page delete.
- **What it writes.** One **kept row** per nearest reading branch: a copy with the same branch, date, life point and `write_order`, plus `kept_for_session_id` = that branch, `source_session_id` = that branch, and an anchor on that branch's copied message (found by origin).
- **Effect.** The branch's pick ranks it exactly as it ranked the original. Its own branches read it through their cut.
- **Then:** a removal proceeds. An in-place change also gives the row a new `write_order`, so the branch's mark now cuts it.
- **Dedupe:** a keep is skipped when a kept row with the same `kept_from_version_id` already exists for that branch.
- A parent's action never forks a child's line and never touches a child's overlay.

**(d) Own-book people (★CV-14).**
- A branch's seats point at the parent's own-book people. In `branchSession`, `settleSeats` passes the parent's seat member ids.
- `findSeatMembers` (sessions/seats.ts:198-227) widens to the own books of the session's ancestry, read through the cut.
- A person forks only when changed on the branch: the write lands on an overlay row in the branch's own book (`overlay_of`, schema.ts:1692).
- The read takes the **overlay chain**: the session's own book, then each ancestor's own book through its cut.

**(e) Deleting a session.**
- Attached-book versions stay. The anchor goes null, and the plain ints keep every cut working.
- The session's own book is deleted only when it was never surfaced (`session_id` still set; `shareAsLorebook` clears it, ownBook.ts:724).
- Before `purgeOwnBookOf` (ownBook.ts:557; sockets/sessions.ts:2098), 🚧 `handOffOwnBookPeople` runs for each live descendant D that reads a person X in that book. It makes X whole in D's own book: D's existing overlay row, or a new one through `insertCastMember`. It copies the versions D reads, keeping dates, life points and `write_order`. Then it re-points through the one writers:
  - seats: 🚧 `reseatOnto` in sessions/seats.ts;
  - speaker stamps: 🚧 `restampSessionSpeakers`, exported from messages/store.ts around `restampSpeakers` (:182);
  - member-keyed state rows: 🚧 `rehomeMemberRows` in state/write.ts;
  - sprite sets: the sprites module's member-set writer.
- Branches made before S4 have no ancestry, so they keep today's sharing.

## 6. Read path (`membersAsRead`, cast/memberAsRead.ts:342-493; one round)
Four queries:
1. Members, plus overlay rows across the overlay chain.
2. Their versions, narrowed per book in SQL: the attached book on the session's line with cuts and `kept_for`; own books at main with cuts. The pick happens in TypeScript.
3. Parent card versions joined to `characters` (`is_deleted`, owner, the highest number for `behindCard`).
4. Sprite sets.

**Straggler only.** A member with no base version reads the legacy way (`castMemberAt` plus `layerMemberAsRead`, :160) and is logged once. The next boot lifts it.

`MemberAsRead` gains the makeup fields, `castVersionId`, `cardVersion`, `follows` (⏳ `followsCard`) and `behindCard`. Tags drop out.

## 7. Back, Forward, deletes
- **Back / swipe.** `retractStateAnchoredTo` (write.ts:702) calls `keepForBranches`, then takes the versions into 🚧 `RetractedState.castVersions` (:862).
- **Forward.** `reviveRetractedState` (:923) and `restoreRetractedState` (:982) restore them with id and `write_order`. A branch then reads the restored row and its kept row: equal rank, equal content.
- **Message deletes.** `deleteLegacyWhere` and `deleteLegacy` (messages/store.ts:364-410) reach `retractLoreAnchoredTo` (:840) through `retractLoreOf`. It keeps rows for branches, then takes the versions, because SET NULL would leave them behind.

## 8. Migration, member creation, the boot pass, lift anyway, S5
**Creation.** 🚧 `insertCastMember` / `insertCard` in characters/versions.ts write the base version or v1. They replace every insert site.
- Members: `findOrInsertBinding` (utils/characterBindingSync.ts:301), availableSceneCast.ts:651, sockets/lorebooks.ts :1214/:1567/:2884/:3574, narrativeGraph.ts :2597/:5305, starterKits.ts:896, providedBooks.ts:1132, tableRegistry.ts:365.
- Cards: sockets/characters.ts :555/:1075/:2268, import.ts :986/:1135, personaCard.ts:231, starterKits.ts :572/:888, providedPrimers.
- Test fixtures: **pipelines/testing/fixtures.ts :485/:503/:541/:579.**
- The insert guard lands with the last insert site, as a closing step of wave 2.

**The boot pass `character-versions`** runs after `seat-members` (startup/index.ts:278) and before `plugins` (:292); provided books then go through the helper. It is `failsHealth`, runs one transaction per book and per card set, uses `tx` only, and has a fast no-op.
- **Cards** get v1. **Members** get a base: retold-ON fields own, the rest `follows`, as in v2 §7.
- **Amendments** are lifted per line, parents first, in amendment-id order, which keeps `write_order` matching `byDateThenId`.
- **Breakpoints.** The breakpoints are each amendment date on the chain within its cuts. The **tiers** are: below every life point, each amendment life point, each presence position, and null.
- **Verify.** For each (line, breakpoint, tier) from the lowest tier up, compare today's fold (`castAsOf` over `amendmentsForAppearance(amendmentsOnLine(…))`) with the pick. Where they differ, insert a version on that line, at that date, with that tier's life point.
- **Lift anyway.** Any mismatch left (overlay rows materialised against their overlaid member, a broken chain, a malformed amendment) is lifted anyway. The pass writes a `note` on the member's base listing line, date, appearance and fields, and adds to a count. The admin index shows _N cast members read differently since the upgrade_, linking to the list.
- **No** lifted member keeps a legacy read or write. `cast_amendments` and the retold columns freeze. The duplicate writer at tableRegistry.ts:644-667 moves onto versions.
- **Idempotence.** A member with a base, or a card with v1, is skipped. Tests:
  - a second boot changes nothing;
  - presences at 34 and 50 with world-dated amendments between them;
  - cuts and branch chains;
  - an attic upgrade, then boot.

**The S5 drop** is as in v2: the data-upgrade `before` hook (db/dataUpgrades/index.ts:41-50) counts what is unlifted, runs the lift core when the pass never ran, and then the drop runs. It also deletes the straggler read.

## 9. Editor, member page, import, Update
- **Card editor: any version, with a picker** (_v1.0 · v1.1 current_). **Save** writes the chosen version in place and reaches its followers.
- **Save as new version** inserts number + 1. The label is prefilled by **bumping the last number** (1.0 → 1.1, v2 → v3; empty if there is none; ★CV-4).
  - The editor then says _3 cast members stay on v1.0_, each a link.
  - **Update ‹member› to card v2** appears per member: on the member page, on `CastLine` and on the Characters row.
  - There is no bulk button and no Undo.
  - Update is authoring. It re-parents only that member's versions whose parent is an older version of the same card.
- **Member page, older version** (★CV-13). Saving says _2 later versions keep their own words_, with **Change later versions too**, which writes the same edited fields onto those later versions on the shown line.
- **Import** (sockets/characters.ts:1347-1395, :1444-1512) compares the file's canonical makeup with the **current version only**:
  - identical → unchanged;
  - different → **Add as a new version** (the file's label, else the suggestion) or **Save as a new card**.
  - Overwrite in place goes.

## 10. SDK API and refusals
- 🚧 `core:query/cast-member@1`.
  - In: `seated-member:` · `player-seat:` · `castMemberId`.
  - Out: `CastMemberV1` = `{castMemberId, cardId, cardVersion, cardless, behindCard, follows, makeup: CharacterMakeupV1}`.
- 🚧 `core:outlet/update-cast-member@1`.
  - In: `member` and `changes: {[makeup field]: value | {follow: true}}`. Review covers `changes`.
  - It writes or coalesces as in §4, and Back takes it with its turn.
  - It is gated by `writes.lore` and the lore write mode. Under Review or Off it writes to the overlay row.
- `update-character@1` (contracts/src/outlets.ts:2062-2087; `CHARACTER_CHANGE_FIELDS`, library/writes.ts:41-48) takes the same makeup names and writes the card's current version. It still accepts ⏳ `first_mes` and `mes_example`, mapped onto the greetings opener and `exampleDialogues`.
- **Refusals** (★S3C-1/2: refuse and reword all). One helper, 🚧 `unseatedRefusal` in state/seatRefs.ts, names the person:
  - _"Bianca (seated-member:12) isn't seated in this session — …"_ at host.ts ~2618 (persona line), ~2806 (form addressee), ~7354 (speaker), and in `NOT_IN_CAST` (sockets/state.ts:239).
  - A book-level annex run (host.ts ~2274): _"A lorebook can't address a cast member (seated-member:12): a book has no seats."_

## 11. S4 interplay
- **Key flip:** `activeCardId` becomes the in-force version's parent's card.
- **Card-less seats:** dropping the filter at seatedCast.ts:48 loses nothing.
- **Starters:** copy members and versions with `follows` resolved, re-own sets and gallery files, and replace `ownCastCards` (starterKits.ts:443-622).
- **Unlink or delete a card:** resolve `follows` first.
- **Inventory A1–A4, A8, B9, B11** become makeup fields or `files.cast_member_id`.
- mundi-27b is unaffected.

## 13. Risks
- **Lift row growth:** tiers × breakpoints. The lift writes only where reads differ.
- **Kept rows and hand-off** have fan-out on deep branch trees. These rare paths need their own tests.
- **An in-place change's new `write_order`** can flip a same-day tie for the parent. That is accepted: the latest edit wins.
- **A Review session's overlay** outranks same-day book rows, unchanged from v2.
- **The ⏳ card-column copies** are a second-writer hazard; the guard tests cover them.
- **Behaviour change:** a branch's entry and value writes leave the parent's line once it forks one.
- **Conflicts** on the migration number and on host.ts.

## 33. CHARACTER VERSIONS — SETTLED DESIGN (v4 = §32 v3 + these deltas), 2026-11-05 late — lead
§32 stands except:
1. **Shape name:** 🚧 **character profile** (`CharacterProfileV1`, `CHARACTER_PROFILE_FIELDS`) replaces _character makeup_ everywhere; a character version holds a character profile. Summary: a separate optional one-liner, readers fall back to the description's first sentence. Greetings: empty greetings kept (today's swipes); notes `{text, lang?, variants}`.
2. **Branches:** no kept rows. One **retire mark** (`retired_order bigint`): Back, swipe, delete or an in-place change of a row some descendant reads (its cut passes it) marks the row retired (`retired_order` = the next write order) instead of deleting it; an in-place change then inserts the new row. A reader passes a row when `retired_order` is null or greater than its mark for that source; Forward clears the mark. Index on `sessions.branch_ancestry` for "who reads this source". Retired rows nobody reads any more are swept when the last descendant goes. The cut keeps `{sessionId, through, mark}` per ancestor.
3. **Deleting a parent:** its never-surfaced notes **outlive it while any branch rides them** and are deleted with the last patron. No hand-off, no re-pointing (drops `handOffOwnBookPeople`, `reseatOnto`, `rehomeMemberRows`).
4. **Acting rights:** `session_characters.seated_by_user_id` (written by `seats.ts`, filled by the boot pass from the held card's owner — today's rule — so nothing changes for existing seats); `messages/permissions.ts`, fire-turn `ownsIt`, `handEdits.ts`, `sockets/state.ts`, `seatName.ts`, `seatPortrait.ts` judge by the seater for character seats and by `user_id` for player seats.
5. **Lift:** the mixed-greetings case lifts as an own list, noted and counted, with **Follow the card's greetings**; the straggler legacy read is **deleted in S4's guards step** (a member with no base is a logged error).
6. **SDK:** `update-character@1` takes the profile names now; `first_mes` / `mes_example` removed (owner: "New names now, old removed").
7. **Still lead-recommended, built unless the owner says otherwise:** ★CV-20 the member page keeps its **Changes** list and words (each row a version, showing what changed from the one before); ★CV-12 no session cut for entries and values (patrons ride the line, per "Sessions do not own lorebook branches, they ride along it.").
Lanes as §32 §12, minus the hand-off pieces in V-time, plus `seated_by_user_id` in V-schema (column), V-core (boot fill) and V-time (`seats.ts` writer + the six acting-rights readers).

## 34. S4 REST — DESIGN (2026-11-08) — the flip, card-less cast, books, Characters view — three Plan agents merged by the lead; challenger next; NOT a go

Owner 2026-11-08: "Plan S4 seat flip". Three read-only Plan agents (key flip · card-less cast · books + Characters view) read the code at today's tree; the lead merged them and checked the rulings they lean on. Nothing below is built.

### 34.0 Where §27/§29/§32 no longer match the code
- §32.11 "drop the filter at seatedCast.ts:48 loses nothing" — the filter is now `cast/seatedCast.ts:129`; dropping it as written admits envoy seats (character null, envoy slug set); a card-less character seat is also refused by `storedOwnerOfSeat` (`state/seatRefs.ts:19-23`), unmatched by `seatCards.ts:115`, and unwritable (`seats.ts:398` "a cardId or an envoySlug").
- §32.11 `activeCardId` = in-force version's parent's card — already built (`memberAsRead.ts:424`).
- The flip list (§27 line 536 + §29 Removal timing) misses: `annex_deltas.before/after` (Back restores from it), zone `entries[].fields.towards` tie ends (`ties.ts:336`, `zones.ts:205-224`), `sessions.genre_fields` keys `speechAutoVoice:character:N` (`speechSettings.ts:148`), and `givePlayerSeatACard`'s rekey to `session_cast` (`seatName.ts:267-330`).
- S3-window data is uniform card form: nothing writes a `seated_member` row today (only `statTrail.ts:24,52` and the doors). The flip is one direction, once.
- S1 never mints a member into an own book (`seatMembers.ts:27-30`; the 11-06 walk: "1 left without one"). The flip needs every live carded seat to have a member first.
- Card delete copies words into followers (`sockets/characters.ts:1174` `resolveFollows`) but leaves them **linked to the soft-deleted card** (sprites, gallery, stats from the dead row; NER drops them). Unlink keeps words (`versions.ts:1258`) but no sprite sets, gallery or card-layer stats.
- Inventory A1–A4, B9, B10, timbre, sprite-set name, face now live in the character profile (`shared/characters/profile.ts:71-89`); A8 is wired (`promptFields.ts:91-97`). `copyOwnBookToBranch` gap obsolete (§33: branches ride the parent's people). `ownCastCards` is at `starterKits.ts:531-658`.
- `files.cast_member_id` (§32 §1 gallery home) cannot work: `files` is unique on (user, hash) (`schema.ts:3613`), so one image can't sit in a card's and two members' galleries. Nothing writes it; only `sessions/backdrop.ts` reads it.
- "Background member" = "a member with no character card" (`lorebook-cast.md`; lore gate `characterLore.ts:139` treats every card-less member as narrator-only) — once starters copy card-less, every starter person reads as background.
- **Book lock hole:** library `attach-lorebook` (`library/writes.ts:473-506` → `setSessionBook` :314) never calls `bookChangeRefusal`; Guide/applet writes can swap a book mid-story today. `bookLock.ts:1-19` and `docs/lorebooks.md:174-176` still promise switching before play.
- Next chapter (`sockets/sessions.ts:896-926`) surfaces the old own book and the new session reads it directly — against pl3 ("Sharing a lorebook doesn't make it useable, but clone able").
- The session overlay is `lorebooks.overlay_of` (`schema.ts:1452`); `lorebook_bindings.overlay_of` (0145) is the member overlay. Folding in the parent folds both.
- Cast-push "~3013" is `resolveOrCreateBindingByName` (`sockets/lorebooks.ts:2972`): makes an unseated member, not a gap.

### 34.1 The key flip (core stops emitting card forms)
- **Emit sites:** 78 non-test files (state keys/owners `state/resolve.ts:1638,1652,1659`, `keys.ts:53`, `write.ts`, `earshot.ts`, rulesets read/ties/zones; seat payloads `sockets/sessions.ts:5460,5471,6749,7175,8319,11293`; turns/speakers `bindings.ts`, `speakerRotation.ts`, `fireAction.ts`, `runTurn.ts`, `host.ts`, `portrayals.ts`, `turnPlan.ts`, `liveScenes.ts`…; speaker stamp `messages/store.ts:117-130` (merges ★TIDY-2's second rule); client projections, `seatIdentity.ts`, turn flow, attendance, `shared/sockets/types.ts`). Card→seat lookups on card columns stay until S5.
- **Rekey rule:** a card-form key maps to exactly what `seatOf("character:<card>")` returns today (`seats.ts:988-1097`), so no read changes: character seat → `seated_member:<cast_member_id>`; player seat → `player_seat:<seatId>` (★27-3). (session, character_id) is unique, so one card → at most one character seat. Ambiguous: card in a character seat AND persona seat(s) (today one shared value set, `resolve.ts:644-649`) → ★K-2; merged pair collides only on `owner_sheets_owner_sheet_uq` / `attribute_carries_owner_slot_uq` (survivor wins, else newest); hard-deleted card / member-less departed seat → counted and left.
- **Stores rekeyed:** owner columns of `attribute_values`, `attribute_configs`, `zone_contents`, `owner_sheets`, `attribute_carries`; JSON: `state_proposals.payload.owner`, zone `towards`, `sessions.annex.core` (turnOrder, turnTally, Crawler `progress.fates`), `sessions.annex_audiences`, `genre_fields` auto-voice keys, `widget_settings.values` (`chosenImages`, `pickMembers`), `metadata.speaker`, `message_parts` `core:blocks` addressee/by, `session_messages.rewind_taken`, `annex_deltas.before/after`. **Left:** `rankings.speaker_ref`, receipts' portrayals (§29 accepted), `session_changes.payload`, Mundi annex (mundi-27b), genre-scoped presets and book audiences (★K-3).
- **Boot pass 🚧 `seat-keys`** (⏳ until S5): after `character-versions`, before `plugins` (`startup/index.ts:294-330`); `failsHealth`, not critical, one `db.transaction` per session (`tx` only), failures counted; one pure translator `memberFormOf(seatMap, ref|owner|key)` in `state/seatRefs.ts` (the existing one door) shared by the pass and every runtime door. JSON stores need LIKE scans — measure on the walk DB; over ~200 ms propose a "pass ran" marker (★CV-9 pattern). **Precondition step** (lead's placement): the pass first mints own-book members for member-less live carded seats (the settle hunk in `sessions/seats.ts` `settleSeats`/`resolveSeatMember`, :300), so the flip never meets a seat without a member. ⚠ An own-book mint counts as play (`seats.ts:300-304`) and so locks an unplayed session's book under today's rule — **this step waits on ★B-5**.
- **Doors kept until S5:** `storedSeatRef`/`storedSeatOwner` inverted (card form in → member form stored: plugins, Mundi's `cardRef` fallback, old tabs); restore paths (`restoreRetractedState`, Forward's tally/annex) translate rows (covers a failed session and pre-flip backups); `unionAudiences` (`lorebooks/annex.ts:240`) per session. A restored backup reruns the pass; SillyTavern import goes through `seats.ts` (no door).
- **Flips together:** one app build + SDK dist. Socket requests wait on `appReady` (`sockets/loadSockets.server.ts:165`; HTTP at `hooks.server.ts:164`), so nothing sees half. Gaps found: `bindings.ts:7897` `spokenRefsSince` without lookup + card-form `who` (:7913-7921) — turn-advise breaks; SDK `senderRefOf` (`turnOrder.ts:183-200`) + `seatRefLookupOfCast` (`speakerRotation.ts:115-140`) resolve a persona line of a member holding both seats to the character seat (needs a form hint on `refOfMember`); client `seatOfMessage`/`messageRefOf` (`seatIdentity.ts:176-232`) need a card column.

### 34.2 Card-less cast
- **Card-less seats.** `seatCast` gains a `castMemberId` form: member must live in one of the session's books and not be an overlay row, else refused in words; `character_id` = linked card or null (⏳ until S5); seater required ("Still theo"); revive by (session, member). `seatPlayer` gains `castMemberId` (★6). `bookCastToSeat` (`seatBookCast.ts:73-78`) returns `{castMemberId, cardId|null}` and stops dropping card-less members. `sessions:create`/add accept `castMemberIds`. Branches: nothing.

Provided books → ★1. **Must not ship before card-less seats** (`bookCastToSeat` would seat nobody) and **with mundi-27b** (phone lanes/presence/avatars keyed by card).

Storage ★9. Independent — can go first.

### 34.3 Books
- **No swapping** (★27-10, 2026-11-05: "All sessions have a lorebook, [own book] or overlay. No exceptions so SDK data storage API is predictable. No swapping lorebooks."). **Wave 0 plugs only the hole:** library `attach-lorebook` goes through `bookChangeRefusal` with today's play-has-written test. ### 34.4 Characters view
- One list, one row per character (owner 2026-11-05 as refined: "the cast badge is probably redundant" → **one badge, Card**, plus a reach line _In 2 lorebooks_ / _Not in a lorebook yet_ / _In ‹book›_ / _In ‹session›'s notes_). Row key = linked card, else the card-less member.
- **Read:** new `cast/characterRows.ts` (cast read module; reads only; `membersAsRead` at the book's present + `cardsNow`); 🚧 `characters:page {cursor, limit, filters, requestId}` → `{rows, nextCursor, requestId}` to the asking socket only; in `GATED_EVENTS` + `socketEgress`; not in `SCOPED_EVENTS` (per user, no scope key); no page pushes — re-ask on open, on `characters:list` pushes, after own actions. Permission filter: own cards; members in owned books; shared `kind='book'` books; live provided books (`providedBooksReadableBy`); **In my stories** adds own books of sessions the asker owns/belongs to (`ownBookAccessFor`); never an admin's view of others' books. Keyset paging on (lower(shown name), key) using stored names (assumption to verify: `lorebook_bindings.name`/`character_id` track the book's present after cv-fix2/3).
- **View:** `CharactersSidebar.svelte` stops loading the unpaged `characters:list` (:1193); filters in the popout (has a card / no card; by lorebook or story; follows its card / has its own words; Favorites, Personas, tags on card rows); card-less members only in story notes hidden until **In my stories**; card-less rows under _Only in lorebooks_ after folders; card row → card detail; card-less → `openCastMember` (`characterForms/openCastMember.ts:6`); ⋯ **Copy to my characters** where the card isn't the asker's or there's none. Rail name ★C-1.
- **User seat manager — PROPOSAL, NOT A GO** (owner idea 11-05): a **Seats** card on session detail, one row per seat (member, active card, who acts for it, live/benched/departed); actions that exist today scattered (open member, bench/unseat, hand the seat, Copy to my characters for a guest) + one 🚧 `sessions:handSeat`; no setting (P3); after card-less seats; M.

### 34.5 Lanes and order (≤2 supervisors live, ≤2 workers each; a walk after waves 3, 5, 6)
| wave | lanes | owns (shared files) |
|---|---|---|
| 0 | **bk-lock** (no swapping + push gaps) ∥ **ner** | bk-lock: `sockets/sessions.ts`, `summarize.ts`, `bookLock.ts`, `library/writes.ts`, `amendments.ts` push lines, host/flows/receipts/announce mentions, SDK `contracts/outlets.ts` if ★B-4, client EditSessionForm/LorebooksWorkspace/LorebookActions/SummarizeLoreModal/SessionViewPanel; ner: `ranking/entities.ts`, `annotations/index.ts`, `keyProposal.ts`, `sceneMentions.ts`, `sceneCastProposal.ts`, `bindingSuggestions/index.ts` |
| 1 | **f-sdk** ∥ **cl** (card-less writer) | f-sdk: SDK registries, S3G's "For S4" list, `refOfMember` form hint, INTEGRATING, golden repins, `sdk:build`; cl: `cast/cardless.ts`, `sprites/index.ts`, `media/{index,access}.ts`, `dowry.ts`, `tableRegistry.ts`, `lorebookDuplicate.ts`, unlink sites, `providedBooks.ts` uninstall, migration if ★2 |
| 2 | **f-door** → then **f-pass** (f-door lands `memberFormOf` in `seatRefs.ts` before f-pass starts) | f-door: `state/**` (owns `seatRefs.ts`), rulesets read/ties/zones/resolve, `seatName.ts`, `creationModes.ts`, `convertRuleset.ts`, `branch.ts`, `sockets/state.ts`, `statTrail.ts`; f-pass: `sessions/seatKeys.ts` + tests, the `seats.ts` settle hunk (own-book mint, after ★B-5); **the startup hunk and the door inversion move to wave 3**, so wave 2 lands code nothing runs yet |
| 3 | **f-emit** ∥ **f-client**, then f-pass's startup hunk + f-door's door inversion → grouped verification → **walk** | f-emit: `pipelines/runtime/**` (owns `host.ts`), routines, `messages/*`, turnPlan/turnYield, liveScenes, resolveVoice, sprites/choices, owns `sockets/sessions.ts` + `sockets/pipelines.ts`; f-client: projections, request doors, `seatIdentity.ts`, turn flow, attendance, owns `shared/sockets/types.ts` |
| 4 | **seat** (card-less seats, server + client) ∥ **chr-read** | seat: `seats.ts`, `seatedCast.ts`, `seatBookCast.ts`, `seatCards.ts`, `rowProjection.ts`, `portrayals.ts`, `conversations/gate.ts`, `handEdits.ts`, `greetings.ts`, `speakerRotation.ts`, `bindings.ts`, `characterLore.ts`, `sessionPageProjections.ts`, `PickSpeakerModal`, `pressRouting`; owns `sockets/sessions.ts`; chr-read: `cast/characterRows.ts`, `sockets/characterRows.ts`, `interest.ts`; queues its `characters:page` types to seat (seat owns `shared/sockets/types.ts` in wave 4) |
| 5 | **st** (starters card-less + **mundi-27b** in the port repo) ∥ **cp** (Copy to my characters) → **walk** | st: `starterKits.ts`, `providedPrimers.ts`, `providedBooks.ts` import, `sockets/starterKits.ts`; cp: `sockets/characters.ts`, socket types, member page + member sheet |
| 6 | **bk-fold** ∥ **chr-view** → grouped verification → **walk** | bk-fold: `foldInParent.ts`, `ownBook.ts` (clone removal), `tableRegistry.ts` (copy into an existing book), `seats.ts` `reseatOnto`, `messages/store.ts`, `state/write.ts`, member sprite writer, `sockets/ownBooks.ts`, menu items; chr-view: CharactersSidebar + list items, Layout/AccessibleShell/bottomBarLabels/route title, STYLE-GUIDE §6.4, holds **NOMENCLATURE.md** for every lane's queued rows |

⚠ **Waves 1–3 are one unit — the flip.** None of them ships alone; **no lasting database boot between waves 1 and 3** (stage 1's between-waves hazard, §33 cv-core). Waves 0, 4, 5, 6 each ship on their own.

NOMENCLATURE.md: f-pass holds it in waves 2–3, cl in 1, chr-view in 6; other lanes queue rows in their reports. Size: L–XL — ~80 flip files + ~10 SDK/core-catalog, ~55 card-less, ~40 books/view, plus tests.

**Tests that gate:** the `seat-keys` second boot (dump of every touched table and JSON column identical, report all zeros), ids-overlap range test (card and member ids share one range — a wrong-person read is silent), Back-before-flip → boot → Forward/Back on `annex_deltas`, one session's failure rolls back alone, attic upgrade → boot, pre-flip backup → boot; door tests (card form in → member form stored; stray card-form row translated on restore); emit tests (byId, owners, portrayals vs audiences, turn-advise, sender of a member holding both seats); card-less writer per cause (prompt/sprites/backdrop/stats match the carded self; second call no-op; same-owner shares files, other owner new rows; guest can open member sprite files); card-less seats (write, revive, outside member refused, envoys untouched, merge rule, card-less speaker gets prompt/voice/sprites/own private lore); starters create no `characters` rows; fold read-equivalence (every member and entry reads the same at every live message before and after; P byte-identical; a branch riding the notes still reads; second fold refused; rollback); Characters page permission matrix + keyset stability; push cases in `lorebooks.memberSessionPush.int`.

**Docs:** NOMENCLATURE §9/§25 (`session_cast` ⏳ retired in data, `seated_member` live, byId key, the pass, fold, surfaced book, `characters:page`, background member), schema comment table (`schema.ts:10114-10118`), INTEGRATING §1c/§1d/§1f, `docs/{session-layout,lorebook-cast,lorebooks,sessions,group-sessions,characters,getting-started,getting-around,plugins,embeddings-and-rag,personas,images}.md`.

**Risks:** a session the pass fails stays half-flipped until next boot (failsHealth rolls the update back); per-boot JSON LIKE scans; members holding both seat forms; per-user hash dedupe limits re-owning files; one-time re-annotation cost; fold's long PGlite transaction on big books and its `write_order`/life-point/capture equivalence; `membersAsRead` cost per page; starter copies flooding In my stories; Guide flows that attached books (★B-4); file collisions in `seats.ts`/`sockets/sessions.ts`/`seatIdentity.ts`/`messages/store.ts` — hence the strict waves.

### 34.6 ★ for the owner
**Ask first — rulings in tension (both quoted):**
- **★S4-1 Provided books' cards.** 2026-11-05: "Plugins can only ship lorebooks as starters. **They contain cast, not cards.**" · 2026-11-06: "Provided-book upgrade → **They stay, with the Update offer**" (built on card versions). Rec: the provided book keeps its plugin-marked cards **hidden from every library** as the release carrier (so the Update offer works), and every starter copy is card-less. Trade-off: cards stay in the DB vs rebuilding the upgrade on cast versions.
- **★C-1 Rail name.** Ruling 12 (2026-11-03/04): the view is **Character cards** (`PLAN-story-playtest-fixes` §12; NOMENCLATURE ledger). Lead's 11-05 reading (§27 line 538): "the rail item likely becomes **Characters**". Rec: **Characters** — the view now lists card-less cast, so "Character cards" misdescribes it (§24.6). Trade-off: the same ~25 labels / ~15 doc lines / ~8 canon rows renamed again.

- **★B-5 Book choice before first play.** 2026-10-21 (`sessions/bookLock.ts:2-4`, PLAN-lorebook-kinds §A): "a session's lorebook is fixed once play has written. Until then it may be chosen, switched or removed" (when the lock bites was left open, B-Q1). · 2026-11-05 (★27-10): "No swapping lorebooks." (the lead then read it as "a session's book is fixed at start; attach/swap/stop-reading go", line 535). Rec: **fixed at start** — the edit form's Lorebook picker becomes a read-only line; this also lets the flip's own-book mint run (a mint counts as play, `seats.ts:300-304`). Trade-off: a mis-picked book means a new session; alt: keep "until play has written", and the own-book mint must then not count as play.

**Propose and wait:**
- **★K-1** `cast.byId` key `seated_member:<id>` (mirrors `player_seat:<id>`; a stale card-key reader finds nothing rather than the wrong person). Trade-off: plugins see a prefixed key, not a number.
- **★K-2** Card in a character seat AND persona seat(s): copy the shared values to each seat (each keeps what it shows today, then they diverge). Trade-off: duplicate rows, history shows two holders; alt: character seat only, persona seat starts empty.
- **★K-3** Genre presets and lorebook audiences holding card refs: leave them, read by card id until S5, then ignore. Trade-off: two ⏳ readers until S5; alt: strip at boot now.
- **★K-4** Unique (session, member) index moves from S4 to **S5's migration** (migrations run before boot passes, so in S4 it fails on pairs live paths left). Trade-off: pairs stay possible (live paths refuse them) until S5; S5 is no longer app-only. *(Changes §27's stage list.)*
- **★2** Gallery home: link table `cast_member_files` (generated migration), retire `files.cast_member_id`. Trade-off: a migration + a registry table vs a pointer that silently loses images.
- **★3** Card delete makes followers card-less (full writer + detach). Trade-off: writes into other users' books; without it the user-delete cascade destroys their sprite sets.
- **★4** "Background member" = **not seated in this session** (lore gate + docs). Trade-off: one member is a speaker in one session and background in another.
- **★5** 0.6 cards already copied by starters stay in libraries. Trade-off: clutter (the view shows them as Card rows in N lorebooks).
- **★6** "Take a seat" with a seat name mints a card-less member in the session's book. Trade-off: one member per named seat, including retire re-seats.
- **★7** Member sprite-set editor + portrait/sprite-fill actions taking an owner (card or member) in S4. Trade-off: ~6 more files.
- **★8** Copy to my characters copies the member's sheets/configs with **current values as the card's defaults**. Trade-off: a story's earned stats become the card's starting stats.
- **★9** `cast_member:` mentions stored as entity key only, no migration. Trade-off: no FK cleanup; readers filter missing members.
- **★10** Member page gets the card editor's controls for post-history, creator notes, likeness, voice. Trade-off: client size.
- **★B-1** Fold copies the whole parent (every line; the session's line remapped). Trade-off: a big one-off copy and long transaction; unused lines come along.
- **★B-2** Button **Merge ‹book› into these notes**; confirm _"…its entries, cast, lines and dates are copied in, your changes stay on top, and from then on the session reads only its notes. ‹book› is not changed."_ Trade-off: "merge" is already a UI word (cast duplicates).
- **★B-3** A surfaced book is read directly only by its own session, that session's branches and its next chapters; everyone else starts from a clone (start form lists surfaced books under Start from). Needs `lorebooks.surfaced_from_session_id` (generated migration, SET NULL — the book becomes ordinary when its session goes). Trade-off: a new column; the owner's other stories can't share its living cast.
- **★B-4** Remove the published `core:outlet/attach-lorebook@1` now (No swapping leaves it nothing to do). Trade-off: a published SDK name — your call.
- **★SM** The user seat manager — draft above; go or park.
